Documents require ongoing revision as new knowledge surfaces from other sources, languages, or times, but existing approaches either edit with no account of what knowledge changed or regenerate from scratch. A KPR integrates new knowledge into a document by extracting claims, filtering and routing them to sections, and flagging conflicts with existing content, producing a ChangeLog that separates what knowledge changes (claim proposal) from how the text changes (document diff). We evaluate KPRs on revising Wikipedia across languages and updating query-driven reports on RAGTIME. KPRs integrate more information and better preserve existing content than rewriting from sources or regenerating from scratch, while adding the most information per token generated. A KPR-revised article also grounds question answering better than a frontier model with search, which does not surface knowledge documented only in other languages.

</details>


### [182] [Capable yet Parsimonious: Extracting and Characterizing Hidden Chain-of-Thought in Frontier Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26637)
*Xiaoyu Luo,Tao Ren,Wenrui Yu,Xiao Li,Qiongxiu Li,Johannes Bjerva*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过标准API的简单自定义工具诱导闭源前沿模型（含GPT-6 Astra）外化中间推理，验证其推理质量并刻画其推理结构特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 前沿语言模型能力提升常归因于推理能力增强，但闭源系统的原始CoT被隐藏，无法验证。作者试图在不访问内部的情况下获取并分析这些模型的推理过程。

Method: 注册一个简单的自定义工具，通过标准API功能诱导模型外化中间推理；先在开源模型上对照原生CoT验证，再扩展到闭源前沿模型（含GPT-6 Astra）；并在竞赛数学、科学和代码生成任务上评估，同时从token效率、推理步骤类型、诱导推理树三方面刻画推理结构。

Result: 提取出的推理在性能上与原生推理相当，并显著优于无推理基线；不同模型在外化、压缩和组织推理上存在系统性差异；Astra表现出token高效的有向推理，更早选择正确轨迹，内部完成基础步骤，仅外化关键推理。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了一种超越基准分数的行为学视角，用于观察和理解前沿模型的推理方式。

Abstract: The rapid capability gains of frontier language models are widely attributed to improved reasoning abilities, yet this cannot be verified as raw CoT traces in closed-source systems are hidden. By registering a simple custom tool through a standard API feature, we induce frontier models to externalize intermediate reasoning. Because these traces may reflect post-hoc rationalization rather than genuine reasoning, we first evaluate against native CoT on open-source models and extend to closed-source frontier models including GPT-6 Astra. We find that the extracted reasoning matches native reasoning performance and substantially outperforms no-reasoning baselines, across competition mathematics, science, and code generation. We then characterize how frontier models structure their intermediate reasoning. Across token efficiency, reasoning-step types, and induced reasoning trees, we identify systematic differences in how models externalize, compress, and organize reasoning. We find that Astra exhibits token-efficient directed reasoning, selecting a correct trajectory earlier, while resolving elementary steps internally and externalizing only crucial reasoning. These findings provide a behavioral lens on frontier-model reasoning beyond benchmark scores.

</details>


### [183] [Diffusion Drafts, AR Verifies: Accelerating Document OCR with Self-Speculative Decoding](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26638)
*Dohyun Kim,Sungjun Han,Hyungguk Kim,Yusik Kim,Jamin Shin,Paul Hongsuck Seo,Hongjoon Ahn*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: GravityOCR 提出参数共享的 AR-block-diffusion OCR 模型，通过并行草稿+因果 AR 验证，减少顺序解码步骤，实现显著加速。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自回归 OCR 模型需逐 token 顺序解码，速度受限；但 OCR 输出强依赖输入图像，适合扩散式并行生成。

Method: 并行扩散草稿多个 token，再由因果 AR 路径验证后再提交；共享参数、无需独立草稿网络；用 GRPO 结合序列/结构奖励优化，避免扩散轨迹似然估计。

Result: OmniDocBench v1.6 上 GRPO 使 Overall 从 94.92 提升至 95.16，接近 GLM-OCR 的 95.48；SGLang 部署每次前向平均提交 9.7 个 token，区域裁剪解码加速 3.94×，整页端到端加速 1.32×。

Conclusion: 验证先于提交的 AR-block-diffusion 设计可在保持准确率的同时大幅提升 OCR 推理效率。

Abstract: Autoregressive OCR vision-language models accurately convert document images into text and structured markup, but require one sequential decoding step per output token, limiting inference speed. Unlike open-ended text generation, OCR outputs are strongly grounded in the input image, making diffusion-based parallel generation promising. However, when several tokens are predicted in one diffusion step, each is predicted before the others are known. Committing them directly can therefore introduce errors. We therefore introduce GravityOCR, a parameter-shared AR-block-diffusion model jointly trained for parallel drafting and causal AR verification. Verifying drafts before commitment lets the model commit multiple output tokens per round without a separate drafting network. The causal AR path also enables GRPO with sequence- and structure-level OCR rewards, avoiding diffusion-trajectory likelihood estimation while updating the shared drafter parameters. On OmniDocBench v1.6, AR-path GRPO improves the Overall score from 94.92 to 95.16 without reducing diffusion drafting efficiency, while the final model remains close to the original GLM-OCR score of 95.48. In an SGLang serving deployment, GravityOCR commits an average of 9.7 output tokens per forward pass and achieves a $3.94\times$ decode-only speedup on region crops and a $1.32\times$ end-to-end page-processing speedup over AR decoding.

</details>


### [184] [Detecting GPT-Assisted Writing Using Interpretable Stylometric Features](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26687)
*Rajesh Kumar,Nabeel Siddiqui,Alexander Fuchsberger*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 本文评估仅从文本本身提取的可解释文体计量特征在区分GPT辅助与独立撰写的学生作文中的判别能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区分GPT辅助写作与学生独立写作已成为学术界的重大挑战，需要透明可解释的检测方法。

Method: 使用90名参与者的数据（每人既独立写作也借助ChatGPT写作），评估八种机器学习分类器，验证时将同一参与者的数据保留在一起；采用SHAP分析特征贡献。

Result: 在留出测试集上，随机森林达到ROC-AUC 0.87、F1分数0.84，假阳性率和假阴性率分别为22.2%和11.1%；SHAP分析表明词汇和语法特征主导预测结果。

Conclusion: 透明的、文本内在的特征能为检测GPT辅助写作提供可测量的信号。

Abstract: Distinguishing GPT-assisted from independently authored student writing has become a critical challenge in academia. This paper evaluates the discriminative capability of interpretable stylometric features extracted solely from submitted text. Using data from 90 participants who wrote both independently and with ChatGPT assistance, we evaluate eight machine learning classifiers while keeping data from the same participant together during validation. On the held-out test set, Random Forest achieved an ROC-AUC of 0.87 and an F1-score of 0.84, with False Positive and False Negative rates of 22.2% and 11.1%, respectively. SHAP analysis shows that lexical and grammatical characteristics drive the resulting predictions. The findings suggest that transparent, text-intrinsic features provide measurable signal for detecting GPT-assisted writing.

</details>


### [185] [Beyond Repeated Sampling: Learning Search Policies for LLM Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26704)
*Ismail Labiad,Matthieu Kowalski,Marc Schoenauer,Rémi Munos,Julia Kempe*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 通过语义级概念引导替代朴素重复采样，并训练小型概念生成器提升大模型推理的pass@k。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型依赖增加测试时计算解决难题，但主流做法是朴素重复采样：独立生成多个解，期望其中一个正确。由于这种方法仅通过局部解码噪声进行探索，常常产生大量近似的重复尝试，而非真正不同的思路，探索效率低下。

Method: 提出在语义层面引导探索：先采样与问题相关的概念、提示或策略，再以此为条件生成答案。进一步将其简化为在单条轨迹中产生大量多样概念的更具探索性的过程。此外，使概念生成可训练：用强化学习优化一个小型概念生成器，使其产生的概念能最大化下游大型冻结答案生成器的成功率。

Result: 在困难数学推理问题上，训练后的概念生成器在相同答案生成预算下显著提升了答案生成器的pass@k，优于朴素重复采样；超过来自更大未微调模型的概念；并能迁移到未训练过的答案生成器，包括不同家族的模型。

Conclusion: 小型模型可被训练成大型模型的有效且可复用的搜索策略，通过语义级概念引导实现更高效的测试时探索。

Abstract: Large language models increasingly tackle hard reasoning problems by spending more test-time compute, yet the dominant strategy remains naive repeated sampling: draw many independent solutions and hope one is correct. Because such sampling explores only through local decoding noise, it tends to produce many near duplicate attempts rather than genuinely different ideas. We ask whether exploration can instead be steered at a semantic level, by first sampling problem specific concepts, hints, or strategies and then conditioning answer generation on them. We refine this into a simple, more exploratory procedure that emits many diverse concepts in a single trajectory, and evaluate it on hard problems where repeated sampling struggles. We then go a step further and make concept generation trainable: a small concept generator is optimized with reinforcement learning so that its concepts maximize the downstream success of a larger, frozen answer generator. On hard mathematical reasoning problems, the trained concept generator substantially improves the answer generator's pass@k over naive repeated sampling at the same answer generation allocation, surpasses concepts drawn from much larger untuned models, and transfers to answer generators it was never trained against, including a model from a different family. A small model can thus be trained into an effective, reusable search policy for a much larger one.

</details>


### [186] [SpeakerMem-R1: Speaker-Centered Dual-Track Memory for Multi-Party Dialogue](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26780)
*Haobo Zheng,Tan Tang,Yan Chen,Weijie Wang,Yingcai Wu*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出SpeakerMem-R1，通过双轨记忆与RL训练的作者模块，解决多方对话中的消息归属与关系理解及状态重建问题，在多个基准上取得领先成绩。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多方长期对话记忆不仅需检索相关内容，还需区分谁说了什么、涉及谁、个体如何互相认知、群体共享信息及状态随时间变化。现有通用LLM记忆系统易丢失人物与群体关系，难以整合分散于成员、群体和时间中的线索，暴露出消息归属与关系理解、以及从交错历史中重建状态两大瓶颈。

Method: SpeakerMem-R1采用双轨记忆：存储带说话人标记的原文消息，以及派生的、组织为个人级与群体级视图的状态；查询时按实体、事件和时间整合两轨证据。为降低结构化记忆构建中的归属与更新错误并支持本地部署，用SpeakerLevenshtein和说话人条件GRPO训练Writer-R1。

Result: 在GroupMemBench、SocialMemBench、EverMemBench上二分类准确率分别为47.9%、69.2%、61.9%；在EverMind-AI公开EverMemBench榜单上达62.33%，为最新SOTA框架中最佳。在全部1,986道LoCoMo问题上达70.85%。305题对照评测中，RL将SFT Writer平均准确率从57.38%提升至68.20%。同时报告二分类准确率与token-F1，消融显示原文轨与结构轨、个人级与群体级视图在标准评测接口下互补。

Conclusion: 双轨记忆结合实体/事件/时间证据整合，以及RL训练的作者模块，能有效缓解多方对话中的消息归属、关系理解与状态重建瓶颈；各记忆组件具互补性，方法在多个基准上达到领先性能。

Abstract: Long-term conversational memory in multi-party settings requires more than retrieving relevant content from long-term conversations: it must distinguish who said what, whom each statement concerns, how individuals perceive one another, what information is shared by the group, and how states change over time. Recent studies on multi-party dialogue benchmarks show that existing general-purpose LLM memory systems tend to lose person and group relations or struggle to integrate clues distributed across members, groups, and time. Together, these issues reveal two core bottlenecks: message attribution and relational understanding in multi-party dialogue, and state reconstruction from interleaved histories. To address both, we propose $\textbf{SpeakerMem-R1}$: its dual-track memory stores speaker-labeled verbatim messages and derived states organized into person-level and group-level views, then combines evidence from both tracks by entity, event, and time at query time. To reduce attribution and update errors during structured memory construction while enabling local deployment, we train Writer-R1 with SpeakerLevenshtein and speaker-conditioned GRPO. On GroupMemBench, SocialMemBench, and EverMemBench, SpeakerMem-R1 achieves binary accuracies of 47.9%, 69.2%, and 61.9%, respectively. On the publicly reported EverMemBench leaderboard from EverMind-AI, we achieves 62.33%, the best reported result among the latest state-of-the-art frameworks. It also achieves 70.85% on all 1,986 LoCoMo questions, which we use as a two-person long-term conversation boundary test. In a controlled evaluation of 305 questions, RL raises the SFT Writer's mean accuracy from 57.38% to 68.20%. We report both binary accuracy and token-F1, and ablations show that the verbatim and structured tracks, as well as person-level and group-level views, are complementary under the standardized evaluation interface.

</details>


### [187] [Agensh: Scaling Organizational Intelligence to 1,024 Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26781)
*Zhihao Zhan,Ting Song,Li Dong,Shaohan Huang,Jianxun Lian,Yan Xia,Furu Wei*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Agensh，一种无中心编排器的可扩展自组织多智能体框架，通过并发 worker 执行异步协作循环，实现了随智能体数量的性能扩展。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多智能体框架依赖中心编排器分配任务和协调 worker，其容量限制了系统的可扩展性。

Method: Agensh 采用无中心编排器的自组织架构，并发 worker 执行多智能体合作循环：持续收集上下文、认领并自分配子任务、行动并分享发现、验证结果、异步合并进度。由三部分组成的智能体组织基础设施支撑：共享工作区、消息接口、共享上下文。

Result: 在五个最难的 ProgramBench 任务上（GPT-5.6-sol high），智能体从1增至128时，平均最终测试通过率从19.31%升至28.78%（约49%相对提升），更大组织更早达到相当通过率。在 pandoc 上从1增至1024时，通过率从33.89%升至55.06%。worker 轨迹显示自组织合作随规模增长逐渐涌现并标准化。

Conclusion: 智能体数量可作为多智能体组织的新扩展维度，扩展通用智能边界，为延迟或时间预算受限的复杂任务提供实用方案。

Abstract: A multi-agent system can reduce latency on complex tasks by executing work concurrently. Several pioneering harness frameworks support multi-agent systems. However, the scalability of current multi-agent harnesses is often constrained by a central orchestrator's capacity to allocate tasks and coordinate workers. To address this limitation, we introduce Agensh, a scalable self-organized multi-agent harness without a central orchestrator: concurrent workers execute a multi-agent cooperation loop, continuously gathering context, claiming and self-assigning sub-tasks, taking action and sharing findings, verifying results, and merging progress in an asynchronous manner. The loop is supported by the agentic organization infrastructure comprising three components: a shared workspace holds proposed, ongoing, and completed work; a message interface lets workers communicate; and shared context retains reusable findings and work intentions. To test the scalability of Agensh, we evaluate it on the five hardest ProgramBench tasks with GPT-5.6-sol (high). Scaling from 1 to 128 agents raises the mean final test-pass rate from 19.31% to 28.78%, an approximately 49% relative improvement. Larger organizations reach comparable test-pass rates earlier. On pandoc, scaling from 1 to 1,024 agents raises the final test-pass rate from 33.89% to 55.06%. Worker trajectories further show that different forms of self-organized cooperation gradually emerges and standardizes as the organization grows. These results reveal the number of agents as a new scaling dimension for multi-agent organizations to expand the frontier of general intelligence, offering a practical solution for complex tasks under hard latency constraints or time budgets.

</details>


### [188] [Flash-dLLM: IO-Aware KV Caching and Parallel Decoding for Fast, Memory-Efficient Diffusion LLMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26796)
*Quan Nguyen-Tri,Mukul Ranjan,Zhiqiang Shen*

Main category: cs.CL

TL;DR: 提出 Flash-dLLM，一个免训练的扩散大语言模型推理加速框架，通过 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核与 KV 缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码，实现快速且内存高效的推理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 扩散大语言模型（dLLM）因缺少有效的 KV 缓存和可扩展的并行解码机制，推理效率低下，实际部署受限。现有加速方法通常将 KV 缓存与并行解码分开研究，忽略了二者联合应用时出现的 I/O 瓶颈。

Method: Flash-dLLM 首先识别出在启用 KV 缓存的 dLLM 推理中，GPU 内存 I/O 是主要瓶颈，并设计 I/O 感知的融合 KV 缓存内核以减少冗余内存搬移；在此基础上，提出高效的 KV 缓存驱动的草稿-验证解码策略，让 dLLM 自身同时充当草稿器和验证器，无需辅助模型。

Result: 在数学推理和代码生成基准上，Flash-dLLM 在推理速度和内存效率上均持续优于现有最先进的 dLLM 加速方法；在 GSM8K 和 HumanEval 上分别相比最强基线 Elastic-Cache 取得 5.1 倍和 11.0 倍加速。

Conclusion: 通过统一优化 KV 缓存与并行解码，Flash-dLLM 实现了免训练、快速且内存高效的 dLLM 推理，在保证生成质量的同时提升了长序列和大批量的可扩展性。

Abstract: Diffusion Large Language Models (dLLMs) have recently emerged as a promising alternative to autoregressive LLMs by enabling non-autoregressive text generation. However, their practical deployment remains limited by inefficient inference, largely due to the absence of effective Key-Value (KV) caching and scalable parallel decoding mechanisms. Existing acceleration methods typically study KV caching and parallel decoding in isolation, overlooking the I/O bottlenecks that arise when cache reuse and parallel token verification are jointly applied. In this work, we introduce $\textbf{Flash-dLLM}$, a training-free inference acceleration framework for fast and memory-efficient dLLMs. Flash-dLLM first identifies GPU memory I/O as a dominant bottleneck in KV-cache-enabled dLLM inference and addresses it with an I/O-aware fused KV-cache kernel that reduces redundant memory movement. Building on this optimized cache mechanism, Flash-dLLM further proposes an efficient KV-cache-driven draft-and-verify decoding strategy, where the dLLM itself serves as both drafter and verifier without requiring an auxiliary model. This unified design enables faster decoding while preserving generation quality and improving scalability to longer sequences and larger batch size. Extensive experiments on mathematical reasoning and code-generation benchmarks demonstrate that Flash-dLLM consistently outperforms existing state-of-the-art dLLM acceleration methods in both inference speed and memory efficiency. In particular, it achieves $5.1\times$ and $11.0\times$ speedups over prior strongest baseline Elastic-Cache on GSM8K and HumanEval, respectively.

</details>


<div id='cs.NI'></div>

# cs.NI [[Back]](#toc)

### [189] [Impact of Data Compression on Downstream AI Tasks: A Study using Teleoperated Driving over 5G](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25290)
*Qixin Zhang,Steven Sleder,Xinyue Hu,Faaiq Bilal,Wei Ye,Zhi-Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 研究传感器数据压缩对远程驾驶中边缘AI任务性能的影响，发现压缩普遍降低性能，但不同类型任务敏感度不同，并找到多模态任务的最优权衡点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶是5G及下一代网络的关键用例，车辆摄像头和LiDAR每秒产生数百Mbps数据，远超当前5G上行容量，尤其在多车竞争时，因此数据压缩势在必行，但压缩对下游AI任务性能的影响尚不明确。

Method: 以目标识别和语义分割作为两个示例AI任务，分别在单模态（视频或LiDAR）和多模态（视频+LiDAR）数据下，研究数据压缩如何影响这些任务的性能。

Result: 有损压缩普遍降低AI任务性能；不同AI任务的敏感度因数据源类型和压缩程度而异；在多模态视觉任务中经验性地识别出一个最优权衡点。

Conclusion: 数据压缩对远程驾驶AI任务性能有显著影响，需根据数据源类型和压缩程度权衡，多模态任务存在最优压缩权衡点，可指导安全远程操作中的压缩策略设计。

Abstract: Teleoperation, such as remote driving, is considered as a key use case of 5G and Next-Generation (NextG) networks. In this context, robots, autonomous vehicles, or other autonomous agents transmit sensor data over mobile networks to edge or cloud servers, where AI systems collaborate with human operators to provide situational awareness and enable remote control. In the case of teleoperated driving, vehicles are equipped with an array of cameras and LiDAR devices, which can generate 100s Mbps (megabits per second) of data. As shown in existing measurement studies, such data volumes far exceed the \emph{uplink} capacity of currently deployed 5G networks, especially when multiple vehicles compete for radio resources. Data compression is thus imperative. In this paper, we explore the impact of sensor data compression on the performance of downstream AI tasks running in edge/cloud servers, which are crucial to alert human operators for safe teleoperation. Using object recognition and semantic segmentation as two example AI tasks, we study how data compression affects the performance of these two AI tasks using unimodal (video or LiDAR) and multi-modal (video+LiDAR) data. We find that lossy data compression generally decreases the performance of AI tasks. The performances of these AI tasks exhibit differing degrees of sensitivity based on the types of data sources and levels of compression. We also empirically identify an optimal trade-off point for the multi-modal vision tasks.

</details>


### [190] [Multi-Agent Video Prediction: Self-Correcting Conditional Frames for Dynamic Scene Forecasting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25302)
*Qixin Zhang,Ajay Kumar,Zhi-Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出多智能体视频预测框架，结合边缘端连续预测与轻量级掩码引导条件帧重构，以应对远程驾驶中动态网络导致的传输延迟和新物体出现问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶中可靠的视觉反馈对安全操作至关重要，但传输延迟严重损害用户体验。纯预测方法在高动态场景（尤其是上行中断时新物体出现）下表现脆弱。

Method: 提出多智能体视频预测框架，含三个角色化智能体：连续预测智能体负责低延迟视觉连续性；车端触发智能体检测新出现物体；条件重构智能体用稀疏掩码引导修复预测器的条件状态，实现外生场景变化的语义恢复而无需整帧重传。

Result: 在真实5G通信轨迹下的基准视频数据上实验，方法提升了新物体的语义恢复能力，同时保持感知质量和实用运行时效率。

Conclusion: 该框架可在网络中断情况下有效恢复新出现物体的语义信息，兼顾感知质量与效率，适用于远程驾驶等实时交互感知系统。

Abstract: Transmission latency significantly degrades user quality of experience in real-time interactive perception systems. In remote driving, maintaining reliable visual feedback is critical for safe operation under dynamic network variability. Although video prediction offers a promising approach to compensate for short-term transmission delays and approximate near-zero-latency streaming, prediction-only methods remain vulnerable in highly dynamic scenes, especially when newly emerged objects appear during uplink outages. To address these challenges, we propose a multi-agent video prediction framework that combines continuous edge-side video prediction with lightweight mask-guided conditional frame reconditioning. The framework consists of three role-specialized agents: a continuous prediction agent for low-latency visual continuity, a vehicle-side trigger agent for detecting newly appeared objects, and a conditional reconditioning agent that repairs the predictor conditioning state using sparse mask guidance. This design enables semantic recovery of exogenous scene changes without requiring full-frame retransmission. We validate the proposed framework through extensive experiments on benchmark video data under realistic 5G communication traces. Results show that our method improves semantic recovery of novel objects while preserving perceptual quality and practical runtime efficiency under network-induced disruptions.

</details>


### [191] [Case for Vehicle-Edge Collaborative Multi-Sensor Data Fusion for Autonomous Vehicle Teleoperation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25304)
*Qixin Zhang,Ajay Kumar Gurumadaiah,Wei Ye,Eman Ramadan,Zhi-Li Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: SHARDED是一个车-边协同的相机-激光雷达感知框架，通过特征级融合降低上行带宽，同时保持3D检测与深度估计精度，并加入自适应特征传输和延迟漂移补偿机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 远程驾驶是自动驾驶的安全兜底手段，但远程操作员主要依赖5G压缩摄像头流，缺乏深度和空间几何信息，难以应对复杂动态环境；而直接传输原始相机与LiDAR数据又受限于5G上行带宽和延迟。

Method: 在车辆与边缘端部署特征级融合流水线，并提出两个机制：(i) 网络感知的自适应特征传输机制，降低上行流量；(ii) 延迟感知的位置漂移补偿机制，缓解不稳定网络引起的跨模态错位。

Result: 相比原始传感器数据，自适应传输机制平均减少50%数据流量（峰值超95%）；在nuScenes数据集和真实5G测量轨迹上，SHARDED在保持有竞争力的感知质量的同时降低了上行带宽消耗和端到端延迟。

Conclusion: SHARDED通过车-边协同特征级融合，在5G带宽约束下实现了感知质量与传输效率的良好平衡，为远程驾驶提供了可行的协同感知方案。

Abstract: Teleoperation provides a critical safety fallback when autonomous vehicles (AVs) encounter scenarios that are outside their operational design domain. In practice, however, remote operators rely primarily on compressed camera streams over 5G, which often lack depth and spatial geometric cues for safe operation in complex dynamic environments. While multi-sensor fusion can enhance situational awareness, directly transmitting raw camera and LiDAR data is impractical due to 5G uplink bandwidth and latency constraints. In this paper, we propose SHARDED, a collaborative camera-LiDAR perception framework that deploys a feature-level fusion pipeline across the vehicle and edge to reduce uplink traffic while preserving 3D detection and depth estimation accuracy. We further design two complementary mechanisms for SHARDED: (i) A network-aware adaptive feature transmission mechanism that reduces data traffic by 50% on average (peaking at over 95%) compared to raw sensor data, and (ii) a latency-aware positional drift compensation mechanism to mitigate cross-modal misalignment induced by unstable network conditions. Evaluations on the nuScenes dataset and real-world 5G measurement traces show that SHARDED achieves competitive perception quality while reducing uplink bandwidth consumption and end-to-end latency.

</details>


### [192] [Objective Video Quality Assessment in FWA-Based Over-the-Top Content Delivery Across Open Source 5G Networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25423)
*Nelson Ion,FlÁvio Silva,Paulo Silva,Ricardo Silva,Mathews Lima,Daniel Luna,Antonio Campos,Augusto Neto,Vicente Sousa*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文基于开源5G测试床，对5G FWA网络中的UHD视频传输进行实验性QoE评估，发现MIMO+256-QAM在5用户并发下仍保持视觉无损质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 5G FWA中稳健UHD视频传输需求关键，但现有研究多基于仿真，缺乏真实网络下网络性能与感知质量的帧级关联实证。

Method: 利用真实开源5G测试床，自动化MPEG-DASH 4K流媒体实验，系统考察传输模式（SISO/MIMO）、调制（64-QAM/256-QAM）和负载（1–5用户），并用PSNR、SSIM、VMAF做全参考QoE评估。

Result: MIMO持续优于SISO，吞吐量与画质更高；MIMO+256-QAM在5用户下VMAF仍高于视觉无损阈值，SISO则显著退化。

Conclusion: 空间分集与高阶调制对FWA可靠UHD视频至关重要，所提可复现框架为Open RAN环境下5G FWA优化提供实证与指导。

Abstract: This paper presents a comprehensive experimental video quality assessment in 5G-based Fixed Wireless Access (FWA) networks, leveraging a real open source 5G network testbed. Addressing the critical need for robust UHD video delivery, the study systematically investigates the impact of transmission modes (SISO and MIMO), modulation schemes (64-QAM and 256-QAM), and network load (one to five users) on both network throughput and perceived video quality. Unlike simulation-based approaches, our methodology integrates automated streaming campaigns with objective full-reference Quality of Experience (QoE) metrics (PSNR, ssim, and VMAF) computed from 4K video streams delivered via MPEG-DASH, enabling frame-level correlation between network performance and user experience. Key findings demonstrate that MIMO consistently outperforms SISO, sustaining higher throughput and superior visual quality even under increasing user demand. Specifically, MIMO with 256-QAM maintained VMAF scores above the visually lossless threshold with five concurrent users, while SISO configurations experienced significant degradation. These results provide empirical evidence for the critical role of spatial diversity and higher-order modulation in ensuring reliable UHD video delivery over FWA. The proposed reproducible framework for detailed quality assessment offers valuable insights for future optimization of 5G FWA deployments, contributing to the practical understanding and deployment strategies for high-quality video services in Open RAN environments.

</details>


### [193] [Spreading Factor Assignment Strategy for Coverage and Capacity Flexible Tradeoff](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25428)
*Luiz Filho,Alvaro Medeiros,Jéssika Silva,Vicente Angelo de Sousa Junior,Níbia Bezerra*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文研究LoRa在多上行流场景下的传输效率，分析扩频因子分配策略以优化覆盖或提升容量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LoRa具备广覆盖、低功耗与抗干扰能力，可连接多设备，但多上行流场景下如何高效分配资源（尤其是扩频因子）尚需探究。

Method: 针对LoRa多上行流传输场景，分析并比较不同的扩频因子（SF）分配策略，分别面向覆盖增强与容量提升两种目标。

Result: 评估了LoRa发送多上行流的效率，揭示了不同扩频因子分配策略在覆盖与容量两方面的表现差异。

Conclusion: 扩频因子分配需在覆盖与容量之间权衡，选取合适策略可提升LoRa多流上行传输效率。

Abstract: LoRa is a physical layer technology with the ability to connect multiple devices in a wide area of coverage, with low power consumption and with interference robustness. In this Letter, we investigate the efficiency of LoRa to send multiple uplink streams, analyzing strategies of allocating the spreading factor for coverage or capacity enhancement.

</details>


### [194] [Tetris: Circuit Scheduling for Rearrangeably Non-Blocking Photonic Interconnects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25434)
*Eliezer Amponsah,Deeksha P Rao,Vamsi Addanki*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Tetris，一种面向可重构非阻塞(RNB)光子互连的电路调度算法，通过优先处理瓶颈端点并尽量保留现有连接，大幅降低 All-to-All 需求完成时间。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有电路调度器多基于“阻塞/严格非阻塞”的理想假设，而实际可扩展设计常为 RNB（经内部 2×2 开关网络路由）。RNB 下建立新连接可能迫使已有连接改路、触发多开关状态变化并带来重配置延迟，使调度问题发生根本变化。

Method: Tetris 基于两点观察：All-to-All 需求常非双随机，少数端点成为瓶颈；重配置延迟足够大以致改变“下一个应调度的连接”。算法按剩余通信与重配置工作量优先调度瓶颈端点，并选择/路由匹配以尽量保留正在进行的连接。匹配保证进展，但连接独立调度，使已完成连接无需整组屏障即可替换，仅在状态改变的开关处产生延迟。

Result: 仿真与硬件仿真结果表明，Tetris 相比基于 Birkhoff–von Neumann 的调度最高降低 All-to-All 需求完成时间 6.6 倍，相比 Sunflow 降低 30%。

Conclusion: RNB 互连在部分重配置下引出多租户调度与路由的新问题，文末对此进行了讨论。

Abstract: Reconfigurable photonic interconnects are emerging as a promising communication architecture for next-generation distributed computing. Yet, most circuit schedulers are designed around an idealized view of the interconnect as either blocking or strictly non-blocking. Practical scalable designs are often rearrangeably non-blocking (RNB), with connections routed through networks of internal $2\times2$ switches. This changes the scheduling problem fundamentally: establishing a new connection can force existing connections to be rerouted, trigger state changes across multiple internal switches, and impose reconfiguration delay on otherwise unrelated traffic.
  We present Tetris, a circuit scheduling algorithm for RNB photonic interconnects. Tetris builds on two observations. First, All-to-All demands are often not doubly stochastic, leaving a small number of endpoints as communication bottlenecks. Second, reconfiguration delay can be large enough to change which connection should be scheduled next. Tetris prioritizes bottleneck endpoints using their remaining communication and reconfiguration work, while selecting and routing matchings to preserve ongoing connections whenever possible. Matchings ensure progress, but connections are scheduled independently, allowing completed connections to be replaced without matching-wide barriers and incurring delay only at switches whose states change. Our simulation and hardware-emulation results show that Tetris reduces All-to-All demand completion time by up to $6.6$x over Birkhoff--von Neumann-based scheduling and by $30$% over Sunflow. More broadly, RNB interconnects raise new questions in multi-tenant scheduling and routing for partial reconfiguration, which we discuss at the end of the paper.

</details>


### [195] [Semord: Learned Semantic-Preserving Placement and Low-Fanout Routing for Distributed Vector Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25514)
*Shengze Wang,Yi Liu,Yifan Hua,Xiaoxue Zhang,Chen Qian*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Semord 是一个去中心化向量搜索覆盖网络系统，通过"语义局部性可路由化"（VHash 语义感知放置 + VecDHT 去中心化路由协议），在无中心协调者的情况下将 ANN 查询路由到少量相关节点，提升召回率并降低延迟与内存开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有向量数据库依赖中心协调者记录分片与向量空间映射并路由查询；但在去中心化场景中，节点可自由加入、离开或迁移数据，缺乏可信节点追踪变更，导致路由信息过时、查询被发往错误节点或需联系大量节点，从而降低检索召回率并增加网络延迟。因此需要一种无需中心协调者、仍能高召回地把查询路由到少量相关节点的方案。

Method: 提出 Semord 去中心化向量搜索覆盖系统，核心思想是"让语义局部性可路由"：1) VHash——将语义相关的向量放置在覆盖网络键空间中彼此邻近的位置，同时避免负载不均，使每个查询只需联系一个小的节点邻域进行分布式本地 ANN 排序；2) VecDHT——一种通信协议，在成员与工作负载变化下维护去中心化路由、区域元数据、抗抖动（churn）能力及 VHash 更新。

Result: 在真实测试平台上的大量实验表明：相比去中心化基线，Semord 召回率提升超过 15%，联系节点数减少超过 60%；其召回率和延迟接近中心化 oracle 基线，同时将节点本地 ANN 索引峰值内存降低 2 倍以上。受控大规模仿真进一步显示，Semord 能扩展到真实嵌入工作负载，并在 churn 下对范围化（scoped）向量检索保持鲁棒性。

Conclusion: Semord 通过语义感知的向量放置（VHash）与去中心化路由协议（VecDHT），在无中心协调者的分布式环境下实现了高召回、低延迟、低内存的向量检索，既显著优于现有去中心化基线，又能接近中心化 oracle 的性能并具备良好的可扩展性与抗抖动能力。

Abstract: Vector databases are increasingly deployed in distributed settings where different users, sites, or domains maintain vector data. Existing vector databases rely on a coordinator to record which shards store which parts of the vector space and to route each query to those shards. In a decentralized setting, peers may join, leave, or move data without a trusted node tracking every change, and outdated routing information can therefore send queries to the wrong peers or require contacting many peers, reducing vector retrieval recall and increasing network latency. We present Semord, a decentralized vector search overlay system that achieves high recall by routing each ANN query to a small set of relevant peers, without relying on a centralized coordinator. Semord addresses this problem by making semantic locality routable: 1) We propose VHash to place semantically related vectors near each other in the overlay key space while avoiding load imbalance, so that each query only needs to contact a small neighborhood of peers for distributed local ANN ranking. 2) We design VecDHT, a communication protocol that maintains decentralized routing, region metadata, churn resilience, and VHash updates under membership and workload changes. Our extensive experiments on a real testbed show that Semord improves recall by more than 15% and reduces contacted peers by over 60% compared with decentralized baselines. Semord also approaches the recall and latency of a centralized oracle baseline while reducing peak peer-local ANN index memory by more than 2X. Controlled large-scale simulations further show that Semord scales across real-world embedding workloads and remains robust under churn for scoped vector retrieval as a decentralized overlay.

</details>


### [196] [Key Reconciliation with RC-LDPC/Error Estimation for Satellite-based FSO/QKD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25646)
*Cuong T. Nguyen,Hoang D. Le,Anshul Jaiswal,Swaminathan R.,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出结合原图速率兼容LDPC码与基于伴随式的误差估计的密钥协商方案，并首次建立评估端到端密钥吞吐量的分析框架，以适应FSO QKD的动态信道。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 卫星FSO QKD中，天气与卫星移动导致QBER剧烈波动，使密钥协商（后处理关键步骤）设计困难，传统方案效率不足。

Method: 将原图速率兼容（RC）LDPC码与基于伴随式的误差估计方法结合；误差估计法在无需额外信息泄露下减少通信轮次；并建立考虑误差估计不完美与动态FSO信道影响的分析框架。

Result: 数值结果表明该方案在各种FSO信道条件下均持续优于传统盲协商，并给出系统参数选择指南；用Starlink LEO卫星与移动地面车辆数据进行案例验证。

Conclusion: 该方案与框架有效提升动态FSO信道下QKD密钥协商效率，并为实际系统参数设计提供参考。

Abstract: Satellite-based free-space optics (FSO) quantum key distribution (QKD) systems have recently attracted significant research interest due to their potential to enable globally secured applications. However, the inherent uncertainty of FSO channels, caused by weather conditions and satellite mobility, induces severe fluctuations in quantum bit-error rate (QBER) between legitimate users. This makes designing an efficient key reconciliation, an essential step in the QKD post-processing, particularly challenging. In this work, we propose a key reconciliation scheme that combines protograph rate-compatible (RC) low-density parity-check (LDPC) codes with a syndrome-based error estimation method. The proposed error estimation method reduces the number of communication rounds without requiring additional information disclosure. Furthermore, to our best knowledge, an analytical framework is first developed to evaluate end-to-end secret-key throughput (SKT), accounting for the impact of imperfect error estimation and dynamic FSO channel conditions. Numerical results demonstrate that the proposed scheme consistently outperforms conventional blind reconciliation under diverse FSO channel conditions and provide practical guidelines for system parameter selection. Finally, we validate the proposed framework through a case study that incorporates data from a Starlink low-Earth orbit (LEO) satellite and moving ground vehicles.

</details>


### [197] [Lizard: Bandwidth-Adaptive Real-Time Video Analytics through Content-Aware Packet Discarding at Last-Mile Edge Routers](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25817)
*Shan Yu,Yu Chen,Yifan Qiao,Sheng Zhang,Ravi Netravali,Harry Xu*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Lizard 通过在最后一英里边缘路由器上根据视频内容感知地丢弃数据包，缓解带宽骤降带来的延迟与分析精度损失，实现延迟降低53.2%、精度提升27.1%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 边缘视频分析受限于最后一英里边缘路由器可用带宽骤降，导致排队延迟剧增，影响时效性与准确性。

Method: 三点核心技术：1) 帧块感知的 RTP 头扩展，解耦编码帧块的数据包依赖；2) 基于优先级的反馈机制，动态评估数据包对精度的影响；3) 路由器上自适应相位转移的数据包丢弃策略，丢弃不重要块的数据包。

Result: 相比现有方法，延迟降低53.2%，分析精度提升27.1%。

Conclusion: 利用视频内容感知的丢包策略可有效缓解带宽退化对边缘视频分析的负面影响，显著提升延迟与精度表现。

Abstract: The timeliness and accuracy of edge-based video analytics can be hindered by drastic reductions in available bandwidth (ABW) at last-mile edge routers, causing prolonged queuing delays. This work proposes Lizard, a system that leverages video-content-aware packet discarding to mitigate the negative effects of drastic ABW degradation that may frequently occur at a last-mile edge router by judiciously discarding packets that contain frame blocks less important to the analytics at the destination. To achieve this, we first devise a frame-block-aware RTP header extension to effectively decouple packet dependencies to encode frame blocks. Second, Lizard uses a priority-based feedback mechanism that dynamically evaluates packet priorities based on relative accuracy impacts. Third, we develop an adaptive phase-transition-based packet discarding strategy at the router to discard packets that represent unimportant blocks. Our evaluation of Lizard shows improvements over existing methods are substantial: 53.2% reduction in latency and 27.1% increase in analysis accuracy.

</details>


### [198] [Rethinking Web Application Firewalls](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25892)
*Laurin Brandner,Laurent Vanbever*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出 Shimmer，一种高度优化的 WAF，通过 JIT 编译规则集并应用高级优化来避免评分流程中的冗余工作，从而缓解 WAF 的性能与延迟开销问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 应用层（L7）DDoS 攻击威胁日益严重，运营商部署 WAF 进行防御。但 WAF 成本高昂，且会使真实应用请求延迟增加最多 4 倍，因此需要一种更高效的 WAF。

Method: Shimmer 将 WAF 规则集进行 JIT 编译，并在评分流水线中应用高级优化以消除不必要的计算。

Result: 摘要未给出具体量化实验结果，仅说明 Shimmer 旨在显著降低 WAF 的开销与延迟。

Conclusion: Shimmer 通过 JIT 编译与优化技术，为构建高性能、低延迟的 WAF 提供了新思路。

Abstract: In recent years, the threat of application-layer (L7) distributed denial-of-service (DDoS) attacks is ever increasing. To defend against them, network operators deploy web application firewalls (WAFs). WAFs are stateful scoring systems which are configured with a rule set that specifies what malicious traffic looks like, and how to handle it. While effective, WAFs are expensive and can increase the request latency of realistic applications by up to $4\times$. This paper introduces Shimmer, a highly optimized WAF. Shimmer JIT-compiles the rule set and applies advanced optimizations to avoid unnecessary work in the scoring pipeline.

</details>


### [199] [Modulating Retroreflector-Aided UAV-Based FSO/QKD Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25928)
*Duy N. Luong,Duy-Tuan Dao,Cuong T. Nguyen,Hoang D. Le,Anh T. Pham*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种装备调制回射器(MRR)的无人机架构，用于BB84-QKD系统，可简化精确跟踪并放宽指向要求，同时推导了信道透射率概率分布以分析性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于无人机的FSO/QKD系统需要高精度指向机制，这增加了系统复杂度，限制了轻量化和能量受限无人机的快速部署。

Method: 提出MRR-equipped UAV架构用于BB84-QKD，建立真实量子信道模型并推导透射率的信道概率分布(PDT)，基于PDT解析获得QKD性能指标。

Result: 数值结果验证了所提MRR辅助无人机用于实际QKD部署的可行性，突出其优于传统无人机-地面系统，并验证了解析框架的准确性。

Conclusion: MRR辅助无人机架构可简化跟踪并放宽指向要求，实现可行的实用QKD部署，性能优于传统方案。

Abstract: Unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs)-based free-space optics (FSO)/quantum key distribution (QKD) systems require high-precision pointing mechanisms. This increases system complexity and limits rapid deployment for lightweight and energy-constrained UAVs. This paper proposes a modulating retroreflector (MRR)-equipped UAV architecture for BB84-QKD systems that enables simplified yet accurate tracking while relaxing pointing requirements. A realistic quantum channel model is developed, for which we newly derive the channel probability distribution of transmittance (PDT). Capitalizing on the derived channel PDT, several QKD performance metrics are analytically obtained. Numerical results verify the feasibility of the proposed MRR-aided UAV for practical QKD deployment, highlight its effectiveness over conventional UAV-ground systems, and validate the accuracy of the developed analytical framework.

</details>


### [200] [Flux: Optimal Scheduling of Optical Circuit Switches for LLM Training](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25949)
*Arno Troch,Seyyidahmed Lahmer,Abubakr Nada,Jeroen Famaey,Michael Peeters*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Flux是一个基于整体工作负载结构调度光路交换机(OCS)的调度器，通过复用电路并将重配置延迟隐藏于计算与通信之后，显著提升LLM训练效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: OCS虽带宽密度高、能效好，但重配置延迟不可忽视。现有工作将OCS调度与计算分离，基于聚合流量需求决定电路供给与时机，这种分离造成根本性低效：忽略计算时间线的重配置会阻塞通信，导致电路利用率低、缓冲区需求大。

Method: 提出Flux调度器，基于整个工作负载的结构来最优地调度光路交换机。其核心思路是复用电路，并将重配置延迟摊销隐藏在计算和通信背后，从而在多种交换速度下都能保持有效性。

Result: 相比传统周期性调度器，Flux将训练迭代时间最多缩短10倍，并将NIC峰值缓冲区需求降低超过三个数量级。

Conclusion: 将OCS调度与计算时间线协同设计可有效克服重配置延迟带来的低效问题，Flux据此在训练速度和缓冲开销方面取得显著改善。

Abstract: Optical Circuit Switching (OCS) offers high bandwidth density and energy efficiency for LLM training, but incurs a non-negligible reconfiguration delay. Prior work typically schedules optical circuit switches independently of compute, using aggregate traffic demand to determine which circuits to provision and when. We argue that this separation creates a fundamental inefficiency: reconfigurations that ignore the compute timeline can stall communication, resulting in low circuit utilization and large buffer requirements. In this paper, we present Flux, a scheduler that optimally schedules optical circuit switches based on the structure of the entire workload. Flux remains effective across a wide range of switching speeds by reusing circuits and amortizing reconfiguration delay behind compute and communication. We show that Flux reduces training iteration time by up to $10\times$ and peak NIC buffer requirements by more than three orders of magnitude compared to traditional periodic schedulers.

</details>


### [201] [Overload-Robust Latency in 5G-TSN: A HoL-Enhanced Hybrid Lyapunov Approach for 3GPP Indoor Factory Environments](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26011)
*Kouros Zanbouri,Md Noor-A-Rahim,Cormac J Sreenan,Dirk Pesch*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 本文提出一种增强队头(HoL)的混合Lyapunov调度器,用于5G-TSN工业网络,通过引入HoL时延项和类别隔离机制,在过载场景下优先保障URLLC等关键业务。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 私有5G网络结合TSN是灵活工业自动化的关键使能技术。无线电调度器需在固定频谱上复用安全关键的控制流量与高带宽感知流,现有调度器在过载时难以保障关键业务。

Method: 在drift-plus-penalty队列稳定性核心上增加显式HoL时延项与类别隔离机制,构建混合Lyapunov调度器。在3GPP室内工厂场景下评估,采用标准3GPP衰落、空间一致性和杂波遮挡,AGV产生并发的URLLC、eMBB和mMTC流映射到专用QoS流承载,进行5-30辆AGV的车队规模扫描。

Result: 在固定20MHz载波上发现约12辆车的调度器无关容量阈值(由资源块饱和验证)。阈值以下,该调度器与最强时延感知基线相当,HoL项使URLLC截止期错失率较普通Lyapunov减半。阈值以上,在2.5倍过载时,URLLC流量比比例公平和时延预算感知基线多1.8倍,99分位时延缩短约4-7倍,以聚合小区吞吐量的一定代价换取关键类别优先。

Conclusion: Lyapunov调度被定位为工业5G部署中一种有吸引力的过载鲁棒机制,可在意外负载条件下保持可靠运行。

Abstract: Private 5G networks are a key enabler for flexible industrial automation, especially when used in conjunction with Time-Sensitive Networking (TSN) technology. In this context, radio schedulers must multiplex safety-critical control traffic with bandwidth-hungry sensing streams over a fixed spectrum allocation. This paper proposes a Head-of-Line (HoL) Enhanced Hybrid Lyapunov scheduler for 5G-TSN networks that augments a drift-plus-penalty queue-stability core with an explicit head-of-line delay term and a class-isolation mechanism. The scheduler is evaluated in a 3GPP Indoor Factory scenario with standardized 3GPP fading, spatial consistency, and clutter blockage, using Automated Guided Vehicles (AGVs) generating concurrent URLLC, eMBB, and mMTC flows mapped to dedicated QoS-flow bearers. A fleet-size sweep of 5--30 AGVs on a fixed 20\,MHz carrier reveals a scheduler-independent capacity threshold at approximately 12 vehicles, verified by resource-block saturation. Below the threshold, the proposed scheduler is competitive with the strongest delay-aware baselines and its head-of-line term halves the URLLC deadline-miss ratio relative to the plain Lyapunov formulation. Beyond the threshold, it degrades selectively where the baselines collapse: at $2.5\times$ overload it delivers $1.8\times$ more URLLC traffic than the proportional-fair and delay-budget-aware baselines with a $\approx 4$--$7\times$ shorter 99th-percentile latency, resolving the capacity shortfall in favour of the critical classes instead of spreading it across the traffic mix, at a quantified cost in aggregate cell throughput. The results position Lyapunov-based scheduling as an attractive overload-robustness mechanism for industrial 5G deployments that must remain dependable under unexpected load conditions.

</details>


### [202] [TopoCompress: Topology Aware Token Compression Algorithm for Distributed Edge MoE Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26061)
*Ning Li,Xinyu Wang,Xin Yuan,Wenchao Xu,Athanasios V. Vasilakos,Song Guo,Haijun Zhang*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出TopoCompress框架，面向分布式边缘MoE推理，联合优化token压缩、专家部署/复制、GPU-CPU驻留与协同路由，通过双时间尺度交替优化降低跨服务器通信。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MoE在资源受限边缘服务器部署时跨服务器通信开销大；现有放置方法只看原始token流量，常规压缩只考虑语义而忽略拓扑路由成本，独立优化导致通信与资源利用低效。

Method: TopoCompress是部署与拓扑感知的token压缩框架，联合优化token压缩、专家部署/复制、GPU-CPU驻留和协同路由。采用双时间尺度交替优化：在线快循环识别并压缩低重要性、高路由成本的token并协同路由存活的专家激活；离线慢循环根据在线推理累积的压缩后流量更新专家放置、复制与GPU-CPU驻留。

Result: 建立了可行性、最优性、收敛性和计算复杂度；仿真表明TopoCompress有效降低跨服务器流量与部署资源消耗，同时保持可控的推理质量。

Conclusion: TopoCompress使带宽和资源受限的边缘基础设施上高效分布式MoE推理成为可能。

Abstract: Mixture-of-experts (MoE) models improve capacity with moderate overhead by sparsely activating experts per token. However, deploying MoE across resource-constrained edge servers incurs substantial cross-server communication as experts are distributed across heterogeneous servers. Existing placement methods optimize for raw token traffic, while conventional compression considers semantics but ignores topology-dependent routing costs. Consequently, independent optimization leads to inefficient communication and resource utilization. This paper proposes TopoCompress, a deployment- and topology-aware token compression framework for communication-efficient distributed edge MoE inference. It jointly optimizes token compression, expert deployment/replication, GPU-CPU residency, and collaborative routing to balance cross-server transmission, quality, and resource use. To address the coupling between token-level compression and epoch-level deployment, TopoCompress employs a two-timescale alternating optimization. In the online fast loop, it identifies and compresses low-importance, high-routing-cost tokens and jointly routes surviving expert activations. In the offline slow loop, it updates expert placement, replication, and GPU-CPU residency according to post-compression traffic accumulated during online inference. We establish the feasibility, optimality, convergence, and computational complexity. Simulations demonstrate that TopoCompress effectively reduces cross-server traffic and deployment resource consumption while maintaining controllable inference quality, enabling efficient distributed MoE inference over bandwidth- and resource-constrained edge infrastructures.

</details>


### [203] [Differentiable Policy Transport over Multi-Layer Network Feasibility Geometry](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26068)
*Zuyuan Zhang,Zeyu Fang,Mahdi Imani,Nathaniel D. Bastian,Tian Lan*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出NFG-RL，用传输理论与残差包含将异构网络约束编译为可微算子，让活跃约束塑造执行、探索与梯度，实现几乎必然可行的学习控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学习式网络控制需满足跨层约束（干扰、功率-速率耦合、流量守恒、服务链、容量、时延、可靠性等），但现有方法仅覆盖部分约束几何且处理间接，如奖励惩罚、拉格朗日乘子或事后修复，难以保证可行性。

Method: 将耦合约束建模为传输理论与残差包含φ(x,a)∈K，把执行策略定义为proto-policy经可行性传输映射的前推。将异构约束编译为带类型的残差块，用可微变分算子传输proto-action，使活跃约束影响执行、探索和actor梯度。理论上证明精确传输得到几乎必然可行执行、活跃约束将探索收缩到可行切空间、critic-tilted传输相对普通投影有一阶非负增益，并可将背压调度恢复为提升漂移残差的梯度。

Result: 在两个公开轨迹条件下的无线边缘代理环境中，NFG-RL相比各环境最强的非NFG方法将可行效用提升37.5–41.5%，原始动作违反降低48.5–60.8%，P99时延降低57.0–75.5%，优于多种优化与学习基线。

Conclusion: NFG-RL通过对可行性几何的显式建模与可微传输，将约束从间接惩罚转为直接塑造策略执行与学习，兼顾可行性与性能，为带跨层约束的网络自动化提供了理论与实验支持的新范式。

Abstract: Learning-based control is increasingly central to automating network operations. A learned policy, however, must satisfy cross-layer constraints on interference, power-rate coupling, flow conservation, service chains, capacity, latency, and reliability. Existing methods typically account for only a subset of this geometry and only indirectly, e.g., through reward penalties, Lagrange multipliers, or post-hoc repairs. This paper proposes \emph{Network Feasibility Geometry Reinforcement Learning} (NFG-RL), which models coupled constraints via transport theory and the residual inclusion $\bphi_{\mathfrak{N}}(x,a)\in\cK_{\mathfrak{N}}$, defining the executed policy as the pushforward of a proto-policy through a feasibility-transport map. NFG-RL compiles heterogeneous constraints into typed residual blocks and transports proto-actions through a differentiable variational operator, letting active constraints shape execution, exploration, and actor gradients. Our analysis shows that exact transport yields almost-sure feasible execution, while active constraints contract exploration onto the feasible tangent space. It further establishes a nonnegative first-order gain from critic-tilted transport over plain projection and recovers backpressure scheduling as the gradient of a lifted drift residual. In two public-trace-conditioned wireless-edge surrogate environments, NFG-RL improves feasible utility by \textbf{37.5--41.5\%} over the strongest non-NFG method in each environment, reduces raw-action violation by \textbf{48.5--60.8\%}, and lowers P99 delay by \textbf{57.0--75.5\%}, outperforming a range of optimization and learning baselines.

</details>


### [204] [ST-NDT: A Topological Framework for Reducing Communication Overhead in Network Digital Twins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26107)
*John Sengendo,Fabrizio Granelli*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 该论文提出稀疏拓扑网络数字孪生（ST-NDT）框架，将网络边流建模为细胞复形上的信号并利用Hodge谱结构选取最小且信息量最大的传感器边，以降低物理网络与数字孪生之间的测量开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: NGN拓扑规模增大，需要实时监控、优化和情景分析；NDT可提供物理网络副本，但持续同步带来通信开销与带宽占用，在资源受限网络中影响可持续性。

Method: 在网络数字孪生中实现稀疏网络监控：将网络边流表示为定义在细胞复形上的信号，利用图的Hodge谱结构识别一组最小且信息量最大的传感器边，从而减少物理网络与其数字孪生副本间的测量开销。

Result: ST-NDT在大多数边监控预算下均优于四种基线框架。例如在20%监控预算（243条边中监控48条）下，相比degree-based、core-number、betweenness centrality和random-selection分别提升10.85%、11.24%、7.36%和6.15%。

Conclusion: 基于拓扑信号处理的稀疏监控能有效减少NDT同步开销，并在有限监控预算下持续优于常用边选择基线，适合资源受限网络场景。

Abstract: Next-generation networks (NGN) are becoming increasingly complex, especially with the increasing size of topologies. To manage this complexity requires the adoption of frameworks that enable real-time monitoring, optimization, and ``what-if'' case scenario analysis. Network Digital Twins (NDTs) have emerged as a key enabler technology for supporting these capabilities because of their ability to provide digital replicas of physical networks operations, enabling scenario testing without interfering with the live network. Although NDTs are considered a key enabler technology, maintaining continuous synchronization with the Physical Twin (PT) introduces considerable communication overhead and excessive bandwidth utilization, limiting sustainability in resource-constrained networks. This paper proposes a topological signal processing framework for sparse network monitoring in NDTs. We represent network edge flows as signals defined on a cell complex, and exploit the Hodge spectral structure of the graph to identify a minimal set of maximally informative sensor edges, thereby reducing the measurement overhead between the physical network and its digital twin replica. Results show that the proposed Sparse Topological Network Digital Twin (ST-NDT) framework consistently outperforms all four baseline frameworks across most of the edge monitoring budgets. For example, at a 20\% monitoring budget (48 of 243 edges monitored), ST-NDT achieves improvements of 10.85\%, 11.24\%, 7.36\%, and 6.15\% over degree-based, core-number, betweenness centrality, and random-selection frameworks, respectively.

</details>


### [205] [Frugal Collective Perception: Context-Aware Adaptive Reporting for Safety-Critical C-ITS](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26470)
*Romain Tessier,Bruno Monsuez,Oyunchimeg Shagdar,Adriana Tapus*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: 提出上下文感知自适应滤波器，动态调整CPM内容与频率，在保持安全性能的同时大幅降低通信量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统CPS以高频广播大量与上下文无关的CPM，导致网络拥塞、处理延迟与可扩展性差。

Method: 基于上下文相关性与情境关键性，动态调整CPM内容与传输频率，优先处理安全关键对象与交互；用SUMO-Artery端到端仿真评估。

Result: 在不同计算能力与传输速率下，安全性能与10Hz最大频率CPM相当，通信量减少超93%，并避免队列饱和。

Conclusion: 上下文感知自适应可使CPS在异构计算平台上兼具可扩展性与安全合规性。

Abstract: Ensuring safety and scalability in Collective Perception Service (CPS) remains a key challenge for Cooperative Intelligent Transport Systems (C-ITS). Conventional CPS enhances perception by broadcasting Collective Perception Messages (CPMs). However, its reliance on transmitting a potentially large volume of context-irrelevant information at high frequency leads to network congestion, processing delays, and poor scalability. We propose a Context-Aware Adaptive Filter that dynamically adjusts CPM content and transmission frequency based on contextual relevance and situational criticality. By prioritizing safety-critical objects and interactions, the proposed approach prevents information overload while preserving timely updates for decision-making. An end-to-end SUMO--Artery simulation evaluates safety, decision-making efficiency, and communication cost under different computational capacity tiers and transmission rates. Results show that the proposed adaptive filtering mechanism achieves safety performance comparable to conventional CPMs transmitted at the maximum allowed frequency (10~Hz), while reducing communication volume by over 93\% and preventing queue saturation. This demonstrates that context-aware adaptivity enables CPS to remain both scalable and safety-compliant across heterogeneous computing platforms.

</details>


### [206] [Reading the Sky to Forecast the Ground: Physics-Informed Link-State Forecasting for LEO Networks at Any Location](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26696)
*Yunxiang Chi,Zhenlin An,Longfei Shangguan,Kyle Jamieson*

Main category: cs.NI

TL;DR: Gnomon是一个物理信息驱动的系统，可在不同轨迹可用性下预测用户感知的LEO下行/上行吞吐量与RTT，并在未见站点显著降低预测误差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LEO卫星网络（如Starlink）链路性能随几何、天气、路由等动态变化，用户感知吞吐与RTT难以准确预测；且不同场景下可获得的测量数据（自身轨迹、邻近站点、仅物理协变量）差异大，现有方法缺乏统一、鲁棒的预测能力。

Method: Gnomon包含物理层与预测层：物理层基于公开天气、轨道、路由和许可数据重建服务几何与四段弯管衰减；预测层按可用数据分三模式——Mode 1用目标终端自身历史、Mode 2用附近公开可访问dish测量、Mode 3仅用物理协变量。Mode 1/2共享微调的时间序列基础模型，Mode 3用紧凑的boosted-tree估计器，三者统一输出接口且无需重训练即可切换。

Result: 使用美国5州9站点、8260分钟1Hz测量评估，训练3站点并在剩余6站点及其服务波束上留出测试。未见站点上，own-trace模式相对最强公开基线将下行吞吐与RTT预测误差分别降低17%和11%，并提供首个LEO上行预测；neighbor-trace模式无需现场硬件；covariate-only模式相对唯一先前的协变量-only预测器将下行吞吐与RTT误差分别降低24.6%和78.8%。Gnomon还提供校准的分位区间，并在重放Starlink链路的真实TCP流上改善自适应比特率流媒体。

Conclusion: Gnomon通过融合物理建模与自适应数据模式，实现了跨站点泛化强、支持上行与RTT预测的LEO链路性能预测，并可用于提升实际ABR流媒体体验。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce Gnomon, a physics-informed system that forecasts user-perceived low-Earth-orbit (LEO) downlink throughput, uplink throughput, and round-trip time (RTT) under different levels of trace availability. Gnomon's physics layer reconstructs the serving geometry and four-leg bent-pipe attenuation from public weather, orbital, routing, and licensing data. Based on what is available, Gnomon conditions on the target terminal's own history (Mode 1), measurements from nearby publicly reachable dishes (Mode 2), or the physical covariates alone (Mode 3) to predict the link state: Modes 1 and 2 share a fine-tuned time-series foundation model, while Mode 3 uses a compact boosted-tree estimator. All three modes expose a common output interface and can be selected without retraining. We evaluate Gnomon using 8,260 minutes of 1 Hz measurements collected at nine sites across five states in the U.S. We train on three sites and hold out the remaining six sites and their serving beams. On these unseen sites, the own-trace mode reduces downlink-throughput and RTT prediction error by 17% and 11% relative to the strongest published baseline and, to our knowledge, provides the first LEO uplink forecasts. The neighbor-trace mode requires no on-site hardware, while the covariate-only mode reduces downlink-throughput and RTT error by 24.6% and 78.8% relative to the only prior covariate-only forecaster. Moreover, experiments show that Gnomon provides calibrated quantile bands and improves adaptive-bitrate streaming driven over real TCP flows on replayed Starlink links.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [207] [TRACTOR Benchmark for Evaluating C to Rust Translators](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25121)
*Hamed Okhravi,Brandt Ogden,Noah Luther,Ian McQuoid,Howard Mak,Nathan Burow*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该报告介绍了DARPA TRACTOR项目开发的C-to-Rust翻译标准化基准测试及评估基础设施。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: C/C++等内存不安全语言导致的内存安全漏洞是持续的安全风险，而语言、程序分析和AI的进步为将遗留C代码自动翻译为Rust（内存安全语言）提供了新机遇。

Method: MIT Lincoln Laboratory作为TRACTOR项目的独立测试评估机构，开发了标准化基准测试，包含递进难度的测试电池和更大规模的里程碑项目，以及评估正确性、安全性、习惯用法和性能的配套评估基础设施和指标。

Result: 构建了一个可系统评估C-to-Rust翻译工具的标准化基准测试，含分级测试任务与相应评估指标。

Conclusion: 该基准及评估基础设施已公开，旨在支持C-to-Rust翻译技术的广泛开发与评估。

Abstract: Memory-safety vulnerabilities remain a persistent source of security risk in critical software, much of which is implemented in memory-unsafe languages such as C and C++. Recent advances in programming languages, program analysis, and artificial intelligence have created new opportunities to modernize these legacy systems through automated translation to memory-safe languages such as Rust. The DARPA Translating All C to Rust (TRACTOR) program seeks to develop scalable techniques for translating large C codebases into safe, performant, and maintainable Rust. MIT Lincoln Laboratory serves as the program's independent test and evaluation organization and has developed a standardized benchmark for systematically assessing C-to-Rust translation tools. This report describes the TRACTOR benchmark, including progressively challenging test batteries and larger milestone projects, as well as the supporting evaluation infrastructure and metrics for assessing correctness, safety, idiomaticity, and performance. The benchmark and associated evaluation infrastructure are publicly available to support the broader development and evaluation of C-to-Rust translation technologies.

</details>


### [208] [TAILOR: Template-Preserving Augmentation for Long-Tailed Log Parsing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25261)
*Sepideh Hodaeian,Zhenhao Li,An Ran Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文研究日志解析中长尾分布下稀有日志组的问题，提出TAILOR框架，通过模板保持增强提升稀有日志组的模板推断准确率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现实日志数据集呈长尾分布，少量频繁模板主导，稀有模板（实例少于五个）虽占比近20%但消息量不足0.01%，导致评测过于乐观，稀有事件的解析性能被掩盖。

Method: 提出TAILOR框架，在模板推断前对稀有日志组进行模板保持的日志消息增强，增加结构证据以区分静态token与动态变量。

Result: 在Loghub-2.0上，TAILOR将稀有日志组的解析准确率较最强基线提升19%，同时在完整数据集上保持竞争力，且增强策略可泛化到不同LLM骨干。

Conclusion: 稀有日志组是日志解析中被忽视的重要问题，模板保持增强能有效改善稀有模板推断，并可与现有LLM解析器无缝结合。

Abstract: Log parsing is essential for system log analysis because it supports tasks such as debugging, monitoring, and anomaly detection by transforming unstructured log messages into structured log templates. However, real-world log datasets exhibit highly imbalanced, long-tailed distributions, where a small number of frequent templates dominate while many rare templates appear only a few times. This imbalance causes evaluation results to be overly optimistic by allowing frequent templates to dominate benchmark metrics, while poor performance on rare yet operationally important events remains largely hidden. In this paper, we investigate the prevalence and impact of rare log groups, defined as log groups with fewer than five instances. Our empirical study on the widely used Loghub-2.0 benchmark shows that rare log groups account for nearly 20% of all templates but less than 0.01% of log messages. Because they contain only a handful of instances, all evaluated parsers experience substantial performance degradation on these groups. To address this challenge, we propose TAILOR, a log parsing framework that improves template inference for rare log groups through template-preserving augmentation. TAILOR enriches rare log groups with template- consistent log messages before template inference. The additional structural evidence helps distinguish static tokens from dynamic variables. Our experimental results show that TAILOR improves parsing accuracy on rare log groups by 19% over the strongest baseline while maintaining competitive performance on complete datasets. We further show that the proposed augmentation strategy generalizes across different LLM backbones and consistently improves existing LLM-based parsers without modifying their core architectures.

</details>


### [209] [The Vocabulary of Flaky Tests in Swift](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25516)
*João Medeiros,Denini Silva,Breno Miranda*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次将基于词汇的机器学习方法用于Swift语言的flaky测试预测，在15个开源项目上训练五种分类器，随机森林表现最佳（F1=0.86，AUC=0.95）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Flaky测试产生不确定性结果，破坏CI信心并延误交付。基于词汇的机器学习预测已在Java和JavaScript上验证有效，但Swift测试以UI和异步代码为主，尚无相关研究评估其在Swift上的适用性。

Method: 通过重执行和commit历史挖掘，从15个开源Swift项目收集91个flaky和22,349个稳定测试；使用TF-IDF unigram+bigram特征，在分层5折交叉验证下训练随机森林、决策树、朴素贝叶斯、SVM、KNN五种分类器，并进行信息增益分析。

Result: 随机森林最佳（Precision=0.92，F1=0.86，AUC=0.95），显著优于平凡基线（MCC=0.75 vs 0.08）。flakiness标记主要为并发原语、expectation同步、错误传播和显式时序依赖；stability标记为断言词汇。模型在flakiness隐藏于共享基础设施或异步构造用于确定性语境时失败。

Conclusion: 研究将基于词汇的flakiness检测扩展到Swift生态，既证明其有效性，也刻画了其固有边界。

Abstract: Flaky tests produce non-deterministic outcomes without code change, eroding CI confidence and delaying deliveries. While vocabulary-based machine learning prediction has proven effective for Java and JavaScript, no study has evaluated it for Swift, a language whose testing style is dominated by UI and asynchronous code. We collect 91 flaky and 22,349 stable tests from 15 open-source Swift projects via re-execution and commit-history mining, then train five classifiers (Random Forest, Decision Tree, Naive Bayes, SVM, KNN) on TF-IDF unigram+bigram features under stratified 5-fold cross-validation. Random Forest achieves the best performance (Precision = 0.92, F1 = 0.86, AUC = 0.95) and substantially outperforms trivial baselines, among them a vocabulary-threshold rule applied to the most informative tokens, confirming a genuine discriminative signal (MCC = 0.75 vs. 0.08 for the best baseline). Information-gain analysis reveals two complementary signal types. Flakiness markers appear predominantly in unstable tests and comprise concurrency primitives (async, await), expectation-based synchronisation (expectation, fulfill), error propagation (throws), and explicit timing dependence (timeout, wait, now). Stability markers, chiefly the assertion vocabulary of plainly synchronous tests (xctassertequal), count as evidence against flakiness. Error analysis shows that the model fails when flakiness is hidden in shared infrastructure outside the test body or when async constructs are used in a deterministic context, exposing the intrinsic limit of lexical prediction. These results extend vocabulary-based flakiness detection to the Swift ecosystem and characterise both its effectiveness and its boundaries.

</details>


### [210] [Dynamic Conformance Testing of WebGPU Through Specification-Driven Mutation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25520)
*Mahya Samdaliri,Zhihao Yao,Kasthuri Jayarajah*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: LANTERN：面向WebGPU规范引导的动态一致性测试框架，通过变异CTS测试发现浏览器漏洞。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: WebGPU CTS只覆盖合规用法，不针对语义边界与对抗性输入；通用模糊测试又因WebGPU复杂图形栈和多进程架构而难以奏效。

Method: 从WebIDL提取显式语法规则，从自然语言规范文本恢复命令顺序、对象生命周期等语义约束；用选定规则指导基于AST的文本变换，生成合法与故意非法两类CTS变体，并在ASan插桩的Chromium上大规模执行。

Result: 在研究者当时最新的Chromium版本中发现3个可复现漏洞，其中之一为堆损坏(heap corruption)。

Conclusion: 语法播种、语义感知的一致性测试变异是WebGPU测试中挖掘浏览器漏洞的有效途径。

Abstract: WebGPU is a low-level graphics and compute API that exposes modern GPU functionality to web applications. While the official WebGPU Conformance Test Suite (CTS) focuses on well-formed usage under the WebGPU specification, it is not designed to stress implementations with semantic edge cases or adversarial inputs. General-purpose fuzzers, in contrast, struggle with WebGPU because of its complex graphics stack and multi-process architecture. We introduce LANTERN, a specification-guided dynamic conformance testing framework that mutates CTS tests using constraints extracted from the WebGPU specification. LANTERN extracts explicit syntactic API rules from WebIDL definitions and recovers semantic constraints, such as command ordering and object lifetimes, from natural-language specification text. Selected rules guide AST-located textual transformations that generate both valid and intentionally invalid CTS variants. We execute the resulting tests at scale on AddressSanitizer-instrumented Chromium to discover bugs. Our evaluation discovers three reproducible bugs, including a heap corruption, in Chromium versions current at the time of study. These results demonstrate that syntactically seeded, semantics-aware mutation of conformance tests provides a way to uncover browser bugs during WebGPU testing.

</details>


### [211] [Evaluating Shaker for Flaky Test Detection in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25528)
*Gabriela Leal,Denini Silva,Leopoldo Teixeira*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个针对Python的Shaker实证评估：在预算匹配的配对设计中，Shaker相比普通重执行无显著检测优势（37.2% vs 35.8%），并揭示flaky测试基准跨环境复用会将真实flaky测试静默转为假阴性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Shaker在Java和Android上报告检测95%的flaky测试（远高于重执行的37.5%），但其对Python的效果未知，本文填补这一空白。

Method: 从Gruber等人的ground-truth数据集中选取非顺序依赖的flaky测试，采用预算匹配的配对设计将Shaker与ReRun基线对比，137个测试各运行100次。

Result: Shaker无统计显著优势（37.2% vs 35.8%，McNemar精确检验p=0.84）。原因：少于一半的ground-truth测试在新硬件上可复现为flaky，且可复现的测试主要源于网络交互与随机性而非并发。

Conclusion: Shaker针对并发的策略对Python效果有限，并警示跨执行环境复用flaky测试ground truth会低估工具召回率。

Abstract: Flaky tests pass or fail non-deterministically on unchanged code, eroding trust in test suites and inflating the cost of every failure. Shaker detects them by injecting resource contention (CPU, memory, and I/O stress) to amplify non-determinism caused by concurrent execution, and was reported to detect 95% of the flaky tests in a Java and Android benchmark against 37.5% for plain re-execution (ReRun). We present the first empirical evaluation of Shaker for Python. Drawing non-order-dependent flaky tests from the ground-truth dataset of Gruber et al., we compare Shaker against a budget-matched ReRun baseline in a paired design, giving both techniques the same number of test executions: Each of 137 tests is run 100 times under each. As configured for Java and Android, Shaker provides no statistically significant detection advantage over plain re-execution (37.2% vs. 35.8%; McNemar exact p = 0.84). Two findings explain why. First, fewer than half of the ground-truth flaky tests reproduce as flaky at all on independent hardware under either technique, and most of the tests that fail to reproduce never diverge once across 100 runs. Second, the tests that do reproduce are dominated by flakiness from network interactions and randomness rather than the concurrency Shaker targets. Beyond the tool, this exposes a broader hazard for the field: reusing a flaky-test ground truth across execution environments silently converts genuine flaky tests into apparent true negatives, deflating any tool's measured recall.

</details>


### [212] [An Empirical Analysis of Cross-OS Portability Issues in Python Projects](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25531)
*Denini Silva,MohamadAli Farahat,Marcelo d'Amorim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 首个大规模Python跨操作系统可移植性问题实证研究：通过跨OS测试重执行和GitHub issue分析，揭示11.2%项目存在OS相关测试失败，构建7大类24子类的失败分类体系，并验证LLM修复可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python虽定位为跨平台语言，但实际部署中仍面临跨OS可移植性失败，且此前缺乏对此问题的系统性实证研究。

Method: 分析2042个开源仓库，采用两种互补方法：(1)对500个项目进行跨OS测试重执行；(2)分析240个GitHub issue并确认102个真实可移植性问题。构建包含7大类、24子类、15种诊断特征、4种修复模式的分类体系。

Result: 11.2%项目出现OS相关测试失败；失败最集中于文件/目录操作、进程管理和库依赖。现有静态分析工具支持有限，LLM识别问题准确率40-79%、生成修复成功率50-77%。提交33个PR，17个被合并，0个被拒绝。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了理解与解决Python跨OS可移植性问题的首个综合基线，为开发者、工具设计者和研究社区提供了可操作的洞见。

Abstract: While Python is designed as a cross-platform language, real-world applications encounter portability failures when deployed across different operating systems. We present the first large-scale empirical study of cross-OS portability issues in Python, analyzing 2,042 open-source repositories using two complementary approaches: systematic cross-OS test reexecution and manual analysis of GitHub issues. Our cross-platform testing of 500 projects reveals that 11.2% exhibit OS-dependent test failures. Through systematic analysis of 240 GitHub issues, we confirm 102 genuine portability problems spanning 95 additional projects. We develop a comprehensive taxonomy identifying 7 primary failure categories - with file/directory operations, process management, and library dependencies being most prevalent - along with 24 distinct sub-categories, 15 diagnostic signatures, and 4 systematic repair patterns. Our evaluation reveals that existing static analysis tools provide minimal support for portability detection, while large language models achieve 40-79% accuracy in identifying issues and 50-77% success in generating fixes when provided with structured guidance. Through 33 contributed pull requests, we demonstrate practical applicability and developer acceptance (17 merged, zero rejected) of our findings. This work establishes the first comprehensive baseline for understanding and addressing cross-OS portability issues in Python, providing actionable insights for developers, tool designers, and the broader research community.

</details>


### [213] [Understanding Maintenance and Support in a Community-Driven Scientific Workflow Ecosystem: A Cross-Space Study of Galaxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25587)
*Khairul Alam,Kowsik Roy,Md Shamimur Rahman,Banani Roy*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对Galaxy科学工作流系统开展大规模实证研究，分析11,762个issue、52,203个PR和6,235个论坛讨论，刻画其维护与支持议题，考察解决结果与时间的影响因素，并揭示跨空间追溯性不足的生态级维护特征。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Galaxy的可持续性依赖软件、工具、工作流、基础设施、文档与用户支持等多方面的持续维护，但维护知识分散在开发空间与社区支持空间，导致难以理解维护对象、维护工件的解决方式，以及用户关切与仓库级开发之间的联系。

Method: 使用BERTopic对三类维护工件进行主题建模，识别issue、PR和论坛讨论的主题；分析协调、诊断、贡献者、自动化与参与等特征同解决结果和解决时间的关联；并探究开发空间与支持空间之间维护工件的显式与候选连接。

Result: 识别出9个issue主题、14个PR主题和14个论坛主题，覆盖工作流执行、数据管理、工具与依赖、基础设施、测试、科学资源、文档和用户支持。解决分析显示各类特征与是否解决及解决速度存在不同关联。显式追溯性有限：16,426个已解决显式关系中97.77%位于GitHub内部，仅294个连接GitHub工件与社区论坛讨论，尽管两空间间存在额外的语义与技术相关性。

Conclusion: Galaxy维护是一个分布式生态系统级过程，研究识别出改进诊断报告、生命周期感知的分诊、跨空间追溯以及社区支持知识复用等机会。

Abstract: Galaxy is a widely used, community-driven scientific workflow system whose sustainability depends on continuous maintenance across its software, tools, workflows, infrastructure, documentation, and user-support ecosystem. However, maintenance knowledge in Galaxy is distributed across development and community-support spaces, making it difficult to understand what is maintained, how maintenance artifacts are resolved, and how user-facing concerns connect to repository-level development. We conduct a large-scale empirical study of Galaxy using 11,762 GitHub issues, 52,203 pull requests, and 6,235 Community Forum discussions. We characterize maintenance and support concerns, examine factors associated with resolution outcomes and resolution time, and investigate explicit and candidate connections among maintenance artifacts across these spaces.
  Using BERTopic modeling, we identify nine issue topics, 14 pull-request topics, and 14 forum topics, revealing a maintenance landscape spanning workflow execution, data management, tools and dependencies, infrastructure, testing, scientific resources, documentation, and user support. Resolution analyses show that coordination, diagnostic, contributor, automation, and engagement characteristics exhibit different associations with whether artifacts are resolved and how quickly resolution occurs. We further find limited explicit traceability between development and support spaces: 97.77\% of 16,426 resolved explicit relationships occur within GitHub, while only 294 connect GitHub artifacts with Community Forum discussions, despite additional semantic and technical relatedness across these spaces. Together, these findings characterize Galaxy maintenance as a distributed ecosystem-level process and identify opportunities to improve diagnostic reporting, lifecycle-aware triage, cross-space traceability, and the reuse of community-support knowledge.

</details>


### [214] [What Was Once Learned May Need to Be Unlearned: Machine Unlearning for Deprecated API Knowledge in Large Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25786)
*Jin Liu,Yanzhong He,Guancheng Lin,Xiao Liu,Jacky Wai Keung,Xiao Yu,Xiaoxue Ma*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文系统研究了针对代码补全LLM中过时API知识的机器遗忘问题，构建了MUDAPIBench基准，并对比评估了八种遗忘方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM预训练语料包含历史版本代码，导致代码补全时生成已废弃API。现有推理时干预、模型编辑或机器遗忘方法受多种合理补全限制，且缺乏对目标废弃行为的验证及对其它API无意图影响的评估。

Method: 构建MUDAPIBench：基于8个Python库、145个废弃到最新API映射、超7000个模型特定实例，仅保留原模型确实生成目标废弃API的实例。在3个代码LLM上评估8种代表性遗忘方法，覆盖废弃API遗忘、最新API生成、其它及无关API行为保持、通用代码生成能力和效率。

Result: Gradient Difference (GD)综合权衡最佳，能抑制废弃API并保持其它能力，计算成本适中。不同库间差异显著；模型训练截止后才废弃的API更难遗忘；逐层分析显示GD以相对受控的内部变化实现有效遗忘。

Conclusion: GD是当前最优的废弃API遗忘方法，行为驱动的基准与多维度评估为机器遗忘研究提供了可复现的验证框架，并揭示库差异、时间因素和内部变化机制。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) for code completion may generate deprecated APIs because their pre-training corpora contain code from historical library versions. Existing approaches use inference-time intervention, model editing, or machine unlearning, but multiple plausible completions make predefined replacements restrictive. Moreover, existing studies rarely verify whether models exhibit the targeted deprecated behavior or evaluate unintended changes to other APIs.
  We conduct a systematic empirical study of machine unlearning for deprecated API knowledge and construct MUDAPIBench, a behavior-grounded benchmark with over 7,000 model-specific instances derived from 145 deprecated-to-up-to-date API mappings across eight Python libraries. Instances are retained only when the original model generates the target deprecated API. We evaluate eight representative unlearning methods across three code LLMs on deprecated API forgetting, up-to-date API generation, preservation of other and unrelated API behaviors, general code-generation capability, and efficiency.
  Results show that Gradient Difference (GD) provides the best overall trade-off, suppressing deprecated APIs while preserving other capabilities with moderate computational costs. Further analyses reveal substantial variation across libraries and show that APIs deprecated after the model's training-data cutoff are harder to forget. Layer-wise analyses indicate that GD achieves effective forgetting with comparatively controlled internal changes.

</details>


### [215] [Confidence-Guided Cross-Modal Knowledge Transfer for Multimodal Anomaly Detection in Microservice Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25856)
*Peipeng Wang,Xiuguo Zhang,Lihua Zhang,Zhiying Cao,Jingchun Zhou,Zheng Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CMT-AD方法，利用置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移，在统一深度聚类框架下融合指标与日志进行多模态异常检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微服务系统的可靠安全运行需要精准异常检测。多模态方法面临两大挑战：模态可靠性因负载波动等因素动态变化；多模态数据在结构和语义上存在异质性，导致难以有效利用可靠的跨模态信息。

Method: 在统一深度聚类框架中联合建模指标和日志，通过软聚类分布估计模态可靠性，将聚类不确定性量化为置信分数；以置信分数引导跨模态交互，用高置信模态补充低置信模态；引入门控中间模态，设计结构与语义一致性约束对齐原始模态与中间模态；并加入模态内与跨模态正则项增强聚类紧凑性、缓解负迁移。

Result: 在三个大规模数据集上评估，CMT-AD优于现有最先进方法，F1分数超过0.9。

Conclusion: 置信度引导的跨模态知识迁移能有效应对动态可靠性与跨模态异质性，实现高精度多模态异常检测。

Abstract: Accurate anomaly detection is essential for reliable and secure operations of microservice systems. While an increasing number of studies have shifted from unimodal modeling to multimodal interaction and fusion, effectively leveraging reliable cross-modal information remains challenging. The challenge primarily stems from two aspects. Firstly, different modalities are influenced by factors like load fluctuations, leading to dynamically changing reliability. Secondly, multimodal data exhibit heterogeneity in both structure and semantics. Therefore, we propose a confidence-guided Cross-Modal knowledge Transfer method for multimodal Anomaly Detection (CMT-AD). It jointly models metrics and logs within a unified deep clustering framework and estimates modality reliability through the soft clustering distributions, where clustering uncertainty is quantified into confidence scores. Guided by these confidence scores, the model actively analyzes the contributions of each modality in cross-modal interactions and supplements low-confidence modalities with knowledge from high-confidence ones. To further mitigate cross-modal heterogeneity, we introduce a gated intermediate modality and design structural and semantic consistency constraints that align the original modalities with the intermediate modality to preserve similarity structures and semantic distributions across modalities. Furthermore, intra-modal and cross-modal regularization terms are incorporated to enhance cluster compactness and mitigate negative transfer. We evaluated CMT-AD on three large-scale datasets, and the results demonstrate that CMT-AD outperforms state-of-the-art approaches and achieves an F1-score higher than 0.9.

</details>


### [216] [When Should Dependency Updates Invoke Repair Agents? A Lightweight Routing Study](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25911)
*Liheng Fan,Jialun Yin,Yuzhi Chen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出DepFixRouter，一个轻量级路由器，在依赖更新PR进入昂贵修复代理前进行预路由，判断哪些需升级诊断/修复，实现预算感知的维护。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 依赖更新PR频繁且大多常规，但少数需要非平凡的兼容性修复。对每个更新都调用仓库级编码代理会浪费模型调用、CI时间、仓库上下文和审查注意力，因此需要在代理之前进行路由决策。

Method: 将问题建模为pre-agent routing，使用创建时刻的文本和元数据信号（如PR标题、bot/依赖标志）训练轻量级LinearSVC，对依赖更新PR按历史兼容性修复可能性排序；并对比回顾性全历史信号以揭示事后泄漏。

Result: 在497个标注的GitHub依赖更新候选中仅72个需实质修复。仅用PR标题和bot/依赖标志的LinearSVC达到0.488修复F1，在前20%路由中捕获51.4%的修复，将每次捕获修复的调用从6.90降至2.68；回顾性全历史信号将top-20%召回提升至65.3%，显示事后泄漏。60例诊断代理试点中，路由门控使实际LLM调用减少66.7%、token减少66.1%。

Conclusion: DepFixRouter可作为常规依赖更新自动化与昂贵仓库级代理之间的轻量级升级层，实现预算感知维护，且部署时路由不应依赖回顾性修复证据。

Abstract: Dependency-update pull requests are frequent and mostly routine, but a small subset requires non-trivial compatibility repair. Recent repository-level coding agents make such repair increasingly plausible, yet invoking them on every dependency update wastes model calls, CI time, repository context, and review attention. We frame this as a pre-agent routing problem: deciding which dependency-update pull requests should be escalated before downstream diagnosis or repair attempts. We introduce DepFixRouter, a lightweight router that ranks dependency updates by historical compatibility-repair likelihood using creation-time textual and metadata signals. On 497 labeled GitHub dependency-update candidates, only 72 require substantive repair. A creation-time-safe LinearSVC using only PR titles and bot/dependency flags reaches 0.488 repair F1 and captures 51.4% of repairs within the top 20% routed pull requests, improving calls per captured repair from 6.90 under route-all or random policies to 2.68. Retrospective full-history signals improve top-20% recall to 65.3%, revealing substantial hindsight leakage in pull-request histories rather than deployment-time routing utility. In a 60-case diagnosis-agent pilot, router-gated diagnosis reduces actual LLM calls by 66.7% and tokens by 66.1%, suggesting budgetaware escalation while measuring diagnosis rather than patch generation. DepFixRouter can serve as a lightweight escalation layer between routine dependency-update automation and expensive repository-level agents, enabling budget-aware maintenance without relying on retrospective repair evidence for deployment-time routing.

</details>


### [217] [Compiling Sufficient Governance Context from Declared Losses and Reachable States: Exact Observation-Contract Synthesis with Cardinality and Cost Objectives](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26016)
*Gaston Besanson*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文提出并验证了"最小充分治理上下文"(minimal sufficient governance context)的推导与认证方法,基于可达状态模型和可观测属性计算充分观察契约,区分单独不可或缺属性与联合充分契约,并在基数或成本目标下择优合成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在治理/合规场景中,需要确定哪些可观测属性足以决定一个声明的裁决(verdict)。现有做法难以区分"单独不可或缺"与"联合充分"的属性,也缺乏在目标函数下可自动推导又经充分性检查的契约合成方法。

Method: 输入有限可达状态模型、确定性声明裁决与候选可观测属性;计算充分观察集,区分单独不可或缺属性与联合充分契约。穷举可承受时枚举所有包含极小的充分契约,否则用SAT/MaxSAT编码求最小基数或最小成本契约,并直接检查充分性。在构造的代码/云域与另一个更大的构造域上做实验,并设计AuthorityBench对比四种基线与三个域。

Result: 在代码/云域中,单独不可或缺核心本身作为观察契约并不充分,存在两个不同的简约(reduct),预注册成本模型能精确区分二者;在更大域中同一模式重现,但该域成本模型无法区分两者(报告为完全解释的成本并列)。辨析度族规模随问题增大而在注册超时内无法穷举,而SAT/MaxSAT在远低于一秒内求解;MaxSAT相对普通SAT未见基数优势。AuthorityBench中仅声明基线在任何域上都不恰好充分。

Conclusion: 方法给出可自动推导且经充分性检查的最小充分治理契约选择框架;每个选中契约均做充分性验证,失败时给出反例、成功时给出检查摘要(非可移植证书)。当前范围为编译器导向的两类作用域,独立的端到端案例研究列为注册后续工作,本文不作声明。

Abstract: We call the object this paper derives and certifies a minimal sufficient governance context: given a finite reachable-state model, a deterministic declared verdict, and candidate observable attributes, we compute sufficient observation sets, distinguish attributes that are individually indispensable from contracts that are jointly sufficient, and select among sufficient contracts under a cardinality or declared-cost objective. An observation contract is a set of candidate attributes whose values determine the declared verdict on every reachable state; an authority contract is one selected under an objective and bound to a gate schema.
