We synthesize every inclusion-minimal sufficient contract where exhaustive enumeration is affordable, and a minimum-cardinality or minimum-cost contract by SAT/MaxSAT encoding otherwise, checking sufficiency directly. On a constructed code/cloud domain, the individually-indispensable core is not sufficient as an observation contract and two distinct reducts exist; a preregistered cost model separates them exactly. On a second, larger, constructed domain, the same pattern recurs, but that domain's cost model does not separate the alternatives: a fully explained cost tie, reported as found. We measure discernibility-family scaling where exhaustive enumeration is confirmed infeasible within a registered timeout, while SAT/MaxSAT synthesis solves in well under a second; MaxSAT showed no measured cardinality advantage over plain SAT. AuthorityBench compares four baselines across three domains; the declared-only baseline is not exactly sufficient on any. Every selected contract is checked for sufficiency, with a counterexample on failure and a check summary, not a portable certificate, on success -- the compiler-focused scope of a two-scope table; an independently specified end-to-end case study is registered follow-up work, not claimed here.

</details>


### [218] [On Behavioral Alignment of Model-Code and Human-Code Understandability via Behavioral Proxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26101)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Anh H. N. Nguyen,Hridya Dhulipala,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文将代码可理解性视为读者与代码之间的"关系属性"，把"读者"从人类扩展到LLM，提出模型代码可理解性(MCU)概念。通过四个行为代理(BPMU: P0问题问答, P1-P3语义自洽的意图摘要)评估多个开源/闭源LLM，发现LLM与人类可理解性行为对齐优于浅层ML基线，且与专业开发者对齐最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前研究多从人本或代码中心视角研究代码可理解性，但其本质应是读者与代码交互产生的关系属性。随LLM在软件工程中广泛应用，需将"读者"概念推广到人类和模型，并探究二者的行为对齐。

Method: 使用含不同参与者群体人类评级的可理解性数据集，评估多个开源与闭源LLM。提出四个模型-代码可理解性的行为代理(BPMU)：P0基于程序理解问答；P1-P3基于程序意图摘要(源自提出的语义自洽性)。进行分层分析与角色条件提示实验。

Result: LLM与人类通用代码可理解性的行为对齐强于先前的浅层ML基线；模型代码可理解性与专业开发者对齐最紧密，与学生群体对齐显著较低；角色条件提示未能改善与后者的对齐，表明当前LLM无法准确模拟不同专业知识水平的人类读者视角。

Conclusion: 语义自洽性是一种可靠且可扩展的行为代理，可用于量化模型代码可理解性，在软件工程研究与实践中有广泛应用前景。

Abstract: Code understandability is a critical aspect of software quality. Prior research has largely focused on this attribute from a human-centric or code-centric perspective, while it should be viewed as a relational property arising from the interaction between a reader and the code. With the increasing adoption of large language models in software engineering, we posit that the notion of "reader" should be generalized to encompass both humans and models. Building on this relational perspective, we extend the concept of code understandability to distinguish between human and model code understandability, aiming to investigate the behavioral alignment between the two. To this end, we use a dataset from a prior study containing human-rated judgments of understandability across diverse participant groups, and evaluate multiple open and closed-source LLMs. To operationalize such behavioral alignment, we introduce four behavioral proxies of model-code understandability (BPMU): P0, based on program comprehension-focused question answering; and P1--P3, based on program intent summarization (derived from our proposed semantic self-consistency). Our findings show that LLMs exhibit stronger behavioral alignment with general human code understandability than previously used shallow machine learning baselines. Stratified analyses further reveal that model code understandability aligns most closely with professional developers, but significantly less with other student groups. Role-conditioned prompting does not improve the alignment for the latter, suggesting that current LLMs cannot accurately mimic the perspectives of human readers with varying expertise. Finally, we demonstrate that semantic self-consistency is a reliable and extensible measure to be used as a behavioral proxy for quantifying model code understandability, with broad implications in both software engineering research and practice.

</details>


### [219] [Post-Hoc Attention Steering of Large Language Models for Robust Code Understanding under Obfuscation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26102)
*Xiaokai Rong,Aashish Yadavally,Tien N. Nguyen*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CodeSteer，通过推理时注意力引导将LLM注意力重定向到语义相关的程序元素（后向切片、控制流路径），显著提升LLM对混淆代码的理解能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 代码混淆广泛用于软件与恶意软件中隐藏逻辑，阻碍分析。LLM虽代码理解能力强，但对混淆代码的鲁棒性研究不足；初步研究发现LLM在混淆代码上性能显著下降，说明其依赖表层词汇线索而非深层语义推理。

Method: CodeSteer是一种注意力引导方法：结合轻量级程序分析与推理时注意力引导，将模型注意力重新分配到语义相关的程序元素上——用于输出预测的后向切片和用于执行推理的控制流路径，从而引导LLM聚焦程序的核心输入-输出依赖关系。

Result: 在多种模型和数据集上，CodeSteer显著提升了对混淆代码的处理性能，常能恢复到接近未混淆程序的准确率。缓冲区溢出检测案例研究还展示了其在恶意软件/漏洞分析和混淆代码逆向工程中的实用价值。

Conclusion: 通过将注意力引导与程序分析结合，CodeSteer有效缓解了LLM对混淆代码的脆弱性，为混淆代码分析提供了有前景的解决方案。

Abstract: Code obfuscation is widely used in software systems and malware to conceal program logic and hinder analysis, posing significant challenges for both human developers and automated tools. While large language models (LLMs) have shown strong capabilities in code understanding, their robustness to obfuscation remains poorly understood. Our preliminary study shows that LLM performance significantly degrades on obfuscated code, suggesting a reliance on superficial lexical cues rather than deep semantic reasoning. To address this limitation, we propose CodeSteer, a novel attention steering approach that reallocates model attention toward semantically relevant program elements, including backward slices for output prediction and control-flow paths for execution reasoning. Our method integrates lightweight program analysis with inference-time attention steering to guide LLMs toward the core input-to-output dependencies of a program. Experiments across multiple models and datasets demonstrate that CodeSteer significantly improves performance on obfuscated code, often recovering comparable accuracy to the level of unobfuscated programs. We also show CodeSteer's practical utility through a case study on buffer overflow detection, highlighting its potential for malware/vulnerability analysis and reverse engineering of obfuscated code.

</details>


### [220] [Why Do LLMs Fail at OCL Generation? A Graph Reasoning Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26122)
*Hamza Attarwala,Moataz Chouchen,Mohammad Hamdaqa,Omar Alam*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该研究将LLM生成OCL约束的任务视为UML类图上的图推理问题，通过实证分析揭示LLM失败的根本原因是图推理能力不足，而非语言因素。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作主要关注提升LLM生成OCL约束的准确率，却很少理解模型为何失败，缺乏对其失败原因的系统性分析。

Method: 使用PathOCL数据集，对六种先进LLM进行实证评估，分析UML结构属性（如导航深度、模型复杂度）、词汇相似度、提示排序策略及图感知提示对OCL正确性的影响。

Result: 随着导航深度和结构复杂度增加，OCL生成性能显著下降；词汇相似度影响有限；UML元素的文本排序会影响性能；图感知提示带来部分改善，但无法消除结构推理错误。

Conclusion: OCL生成主要受限于图推理能力而非纯语言因素，结构推理是当前LLM在模型驱动工程任务中的关键瓶颈。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are increasingly used to generate Object Constraint Language (OCL) constraints from natural language specifications and UML class diagrams. However, existing work mainly focuses on improving accuracy, with limited understanding of why these models fail. Aims. This study investigates the underlying causes of LLM failures in OCL generation, framing the task as a graph reasoning problem over UML class diagrams. Method. We conduct an empirical evaluation using the PathOCL dataset across six state-of-the-art LLMs. We analyze the impact of UML structural properties (e.g., navigation depth and model complexity), lexical similarity, prompt ordering strategies, and graph-aware prompting on OCL correctness. Results. We find that OCL generation performance significantly degrades with increasing navigation depth and structural complexity. Lexical similarity has limited influence, while textual ordering of UML elements affects performance. Graph-based prompting yields partial improvements but does not eliminate structural reasoning errors. Conclusions. OCL generation is primarily constrained by graph reasoning limitations rather than purely linguistic factors. These results highlight structural reasoning as a key bottleneck for current LLMs in model-driven engineering tasks.

</details>


### [221] [A Large-Scale Longitudinal Study of Multi-CI Service Adoption](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26181)
*Taher A. Ghaleb,Rasha Bin-Thalab*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文对13万余GitHub仓库、7种语言、8个CI服务进行纵向研究，刻画多CI服务的采用、演化与弃用生命周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CI服务已从单一实践演变为多服务竞争格局，许多项目不再依赖单一CI服务，但多服务行为在项目生命周期中的演化仍不明确。既有研究多聚焦单一服务、特定语言或访谈，多服务使用生命周期研究不足。

Method: 对135227个GitHub仓库（7种语言、8个CI服务）开展大规模纵向研究，结合定量与定性分析，刻画CI服务的采用、演化与弃用。

Result: 约五分之一仓库使用多个CI服务，多为迁移过渡而非长期并行；CI服务选择是采用、复杂度、维护与参与度的主导因素，语言影响有限。2020年GitHub Actions转型为平台级同步迁移，超50%首次采用；Travis CI涨价后弃用信号最强。仅约11%提交说明战略性原因。

Conclusion: 多CI采用主要与项目成熟度相关，弃用更与服务身份相关而非仓库特征；为CI工具、迁移支持及未来研究提供实证依据。

Abstract: Continuous Integration (CI) adoption has evolved from a single practice into a broad landscape of competing services, and many projects no longer rely on a single CI service. However, how this multi-service behavior evolves over a project's lifetime remains unknown. Prior work has examined CI adoption and migration through individual services, specific languages, or developer interviews, leaving the lifecycle of multi-service usage underexplored. In this paper, we conduct a large-scale longitudinal study of multi-CI service adoption covering 135227 GitHub repositories from seven programming languages and involving eight CI services. We characterize how CI services are adopted, evolved, and abandoned over time through quantitative and qualitative analyses. About one in five repositories use multiple CI services, often transitional during migration rather than for sustained parallel use. CI service choice is the dominant factor across adoption, complexity, maintenance, and engagement, with language effects limited to specific cases. Our results reveal that the shift to GitHub Actions was a synchronized platform-level transition, crossing 50\% of first adoptions in 2020 across all studied languages, while the decline of Travis CI following its pricing change became the strongest abandonment signal. Our analysis of CI configuration rationales shows that activity is mostly reactive: only about 11\% of commits state a strategic reason, indicating that commit messages capture operational maintenance patterns more reliably than strategic intent. Finally, we find that multi-CI adoption is primarily associated with project maturity, whereas abandonment is more strongly associated with service identity than repository characteristics. These findings provide empirical evidence for CI tooling, migration support, and future research on managing evolving software delivery infrastructure.

</details>


### [222] [WatchPoint: Executable User Feedback for Real-World Agentic Web Development](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26204)
*Guanqun Yang,Wei Yang,Xueqing Liu*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出 WatchPoint——一个模拟开发者的"用户"系统：通过生成并执行诊断脚本与运行中的应用交互，产出结构化观测以指导编码模型的再次尝试，在 Web-Bench 上恢复 57.6% 的任务，接近人类测试者的 54.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有编码智能体的反馈机制多依赖截图、LLM 评分或自然语言纠正，缺乏像真人开发者那样直接与在线应用交互（点击、检查样式、运行诊断命令）的能力，因此难以真正定位失败原因。

Method: WatchPoint 生成并执行针对运行中应用的诊断脚本，模拟真实开发者行为，产出结构化观测作为反馈指导模型重试；评测在 Web-Bench 上进行（50 个多文件 Web 项目、1000 个顺序依赖任务，由确定性端到端测试验证），并开展对照用户研究，与人类测试者对比。

Result: WatchPoint 恢复其诊断任务的 57.6%，人类测试者恢复率为 54.5%，表明自动诊断脚本可替代交互式人工测试；同时识别出一类能力缺口模式，界定模拟用户反馈何时有帮助、何时应被抑制。

Conclusion: 模拟用户式诊断脚本能在顺序化 Web 开发任务上达到接近人类的反馈效果，并可通过能力缺口模式判断反馈的适用性，为编码智能体的反馈机制提供新方向。

Abstract: When a professional web developer's code fails a test, they do not simply re-read the stack trace. They open the application in a browser, click buttons, inspect computed styles, and run diagnostic commands to understand what went wrong. Existing feedback mechanisms for coding agents rely on screenshots, LLM-as-a-judge scoring, or natural-language corrections, but few interact with the live application the way a developer would. We introduce WatchPoint, a simulated-user system that mimics real developer behavior by generating and executing diagnostic scripts against the running application, producing structured observations that guide the coding model's retry. Unlike prior approaches that target single-file edits or evaluate using non-executable metrics, we operate on Web-Bench, a benchmark of 50 multi-file web projects comprising 1,000 sequentially dependent tasks, verified by deterministic end-to-end tests. WatchPoint recovers 57.6% of the tasks it diagnoses, and a controlled user study confirms the simulation's realism: human testers achieve a comparable recovery rate (54.5%), providing evidence that automated diagnostic scripts can substitute for interactive human testing on sequential web development tasks. We further identify a pattern of capability gaps that governs when simulated-user feedback is helpful and when it should be withheld.

</details>


### [223] [Reducing Hallucinations in Large Language Models Through Integrated Self-Verification and Retrieval-Augmented Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26229)
*Ashly Joseph*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 提出CoVe-RAG+框架，融合链式验证与检索增强生成，降低LLM在工程任务中的幻觉。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于CAD文档、标准合规验证、知识检索等高端工程任务，但易产生幻觉，输出看似可信却无依据，影响精度与合规至关重要的工程应用的可信度。

Method: CoVe-RAG+统一框架，将链式验证(CoVe)与检索增强生成(RAG)结合；在工程标准、CAD信息、仿真报告等权威外部来源中支持LLM验证，并通过迭代式自验证检验关键论断。

Result: 在CAD模型文档、标准合规验证、历史设计数据复用等任务上评估，事实准确率相比基线CoVe和RAG方法提升28%；通过提供可解释的验证报告与来源溯源增强用户信任。

Conclusion: CoVe-RAG+为事实准确性至关重要的工程设计流程提供了可扩展且可靠的LLM应用方案。

Abstract: Large Language Models (LLMs) are progressively used for advanced engineering tasks, includes Computer-Aided Design (CAD) documentation, standards compliance verification, and knowledge retrieval. Still, they are prone to produce hallucinations, outputs that seem convincing but aren't based on context that limit their trustworthiness in high-end engineering applications where precision and compliance are crucial. The paper introduces CoVe-RAG+, a unified framework that integrates Chain-of-Verification (CoVe) with Retrieval-Augmented Generation (RAG) to mitigate hallucinations in the results generated by large language models (LLMs). CoVe-RAG+ supports LLM verification in external sources of authority, such as engineering standards, CAD information, and simulation reports, while applying an iterative self-verification process to validate important claims. CoVe-RAG+ is assessed on engineering activities such as CAD model documentation, standards compliance verification, and the reutilization of historical design data. Experimental findings indicate a 28% improvement in factual accuracy relative to baseline CoVe and RAG methodologies. Moreover, CoVe-RAG+ strengthens user confidence by providing elucidative verification reports and source traceability. The findings indicate that CoVe-RAG+ provides a scalable and reliable option for implementing LLMs in engineering design processes where factual accuracy is critical.

</details>


### [224] [CANcept: Model-based CAN Traffic Generation and Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26263)
*Lino Wertz,Junes Sheikhi,Florian Fehrle,Adrian Rupp,Tianhai Liu,Philipp Kern,Carsten Sinz,Bernhard Beckert*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出一个开源模型驱动工具，用于规范、生成、回放和操纵CAN总线流量，通过流量调度模型与DBC通信模型实现精确时序控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 测试基于CAN总线的软件时序安全属性需要精确控制传输数据和通信时序，但现有开源工具通常用底层脚本编码场景，难以维护和演进。

Method: 工具包含两部分模型：流量调度模型（TSM）定义消息时序与内容变换；基于DBC的通信模型（DCM）定义消息级流量与CAN帧之间的编码解码。两者结合在同一执行机制中完成流量生成、操纵及变换后轨迹回放。

Result: 初步评估表明，该工具实现了指定场景，时序偏差低，在高流量速率下执行稳定。

Conclusion: 该开源模型驱动工具可有效支持CAN总线时序相关安全属性的测试需求。

Abstract: Testing timing-related safety properties of Controller Area Network (CAN)-based software requires precise control over transmitted data and communication timing. Existing open-source tools typically encode such scenarios in low-level scripts, making them difficult to maintain and evolve. We present \toolName{}, an open-source model-based tool for specifying, generating, replaying, and manipulating CAN traffic. A traffic schedule model (TSM) defines message timing and content transformations, and a DBC-based communication model (DCM) defines encoding and decoding between message-level traffic and CAN frames. \toolName{} combines both models in one execution mechanism for generated and manipulated traffic, including replay of transformed traces. A preliminary evaluation indicates that \toolName{} realizes the specified scenarios, with low timing deviation and stable execution under elevated traffic rates.

</details>


### [225] [On the Lexical Superstition of Large Language Models for Code Comprehension: Re-evaluation on Code of Low Lexical Quality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26388)
*Xin Shen,San-Zhuo Xi,Yali Du,Ming Li*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出Face/Off标识符重命名框架，发现当前LLM在代码理解任务中过度依赖词法线索（标识符名称），即使程序结构不变；性能随标识符信息移除或误导而下降，且该问题在prompt和微调干预下依然存在。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM广泛用于代码任务，而标识符名称在自然代码中统计信息丰富却并不总可靠。作者想探究：在保持程序结构不变的前提下，LLM是否对词法线索赋予了不成比例的权重。

Method: 提出Face/Off——一个语义保持的标识符重命名框架，在多个模型和代码理解任务上评估递进式命名条件（逐步移除或误导标识符信息）。并测试代表性prompt与微调干预，以及类型推断对照实验作为边界验证。

Result: 词法过度加权在所评估模型和主要任务中普遍存在：性能通常随标识符信息被移除或误导而下降，输出常被导向误导性名称所暗示的含义；在prompt和微调干预下该模式仍持续。类型推断对照显示：当答案无需目标名称即可本地恢复时，命名效应较小。

Conclusion: 结果并不意味着标识符无用，而是揭示当前LLM在平衡词法线索与程序结构方面存在系统性脆弱性。这促使我们开发既保留自然代码规律优势、又让结论基于准确形式化代码语义的评估与建模方法。

Abstract: Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) have made them widely used for code-related tasks. Identifier names are statistically informative in naturally occurring code, but their information is not always reliable. We investigate whether current LLMs assign disproportionate weight to lexical cues when renaming preserves program structure. We introduce Face/Off, a semantics-preserving identifier-renaming framework, and evaluate progressive naming conditions across multiple models and code-comprehension tasks. Within this framework, lexical overemphasis is pervasive across the evaluated models and primary tasks: performance generally decreases as identifier information is removed or made misleading, and outputs are often directed toward the meanings suggested by misleading names. The pattern persists under representative prompt- and fine-tuning-based interventions, suggesting that lexical overemphasis is an entrenched problem. A type-inference control confirms a boundary: naming effects are smaller when the answer is locally recoverable without the target name. These results do not imply that identifiers are unhelpful; rather, they reveal a systematic vulnerability in how current LLMs balance lexical cues against program structure. Our findings motivate evaluations and modeling methods that preserve the benefits of natural code regularities while keeping conclusions grounded in accurate, formalized code semantics.

</details>


### [226] [FeatLens: Feature-Guided Dynamic Code Graph Construction and Retrieval for Repository-Level Code Generation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26480)
*Xutian Li,Bo Xiong,Yifeng Zhu,Kunze Li,Xianlin Zhao,Runbang Yan,Yanzhen Zou,Lu Zhang,Bing Xie*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: FeatLens 提出一种特性引导的动态代码图构建与检索方法，用于仓库级代码生成，通过特性索引动态构建任务专属图并用个性化 PageRank 选取紧凑推理图，在低开销下提升依赖检索与生成效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 仓库级代码生成需识别可复用的仓库依赖（函数、API、跨文件定义）。现有检索方法（代码相似度、持久化全仓库图、LLM 图探索）构建、推理与 token 开销高；特性导向方法虽反映软件功能，却主要用于需求分解、规划或特性编辑，而非代码依赖检索。

Method: FeatLens 构建特性索引，将自然语言特性描述链接到函数级代码实体。给定生成任务，它从特性索引动态构建任务专属种子图，并通过语义-结构图推理与个性化 PageRank 选出紧凑推理图，从而以确定性、轻量方式替代持久化全仓库图维护与 LLM 探索。

Result: 在 DevEval 和 EvoCodeBench 上，FeatLens 在稀疏、稠密与图基线中取得最佳 DR@15（0.501 与 0.460）。在 DevEval 生成上获得最高 DIR@1：DeepSeek-V3.2 达 52.91%，GPT-5-mini 达 53.58%，同时保持有竞争力的 Pass@1 并生成更短代码。相比最强图基线，图节点减少 61.0%、边减少 86.2%、总 token 开销减少 45.9%，检索阶段不使用 LLM token。

Conclusion: FeatLens 证明特性引导的动态代码图检索可有效替代高成本的全仓库图或 LLM 探索方式，在提升仓库级代码生成依赖检索与生成质量的同时显著降低图规模与 token 开销。

Abstract: Recent code generation research has moved from isolated function completion toward repository-level generation in existing codebases. To implement a target function correctly, an LLM must identify reusable repository dependencies such as existing functions, APIs, and cross-file definitions. Existing retrieval methods provide such context through code similarity search, persistent whole-repository graphs, or LLM-driven graph exploration, but often incur high graph construction, reasoning, and token costs. Feature-oriented methods offer a natural view of software functionality, yet they mainly support requirement decomposition, planning, or feature editing rather than code dependency retrieval. This paper presents \textbf{FeatLens}, a feature-guided dynamic code graph construction and retrieval approach for repository-level code generation. FeatLens builds a feature index that links natural-language feature descriptions to function-level code entities. Given a generation task, it dynamically constructs a task-specific seed graph from the feature index and applies semantic-structural graph reasoning with personalized PageRank to select a compact reasoning graph. This design replaces persistent whole-repository graph maintenance and LLM exploration with deterministic and lightweight dependency retrieval. Experiments on DevEval and EvoCodeBench show that FeatLens achieves the best DR@15 among sparse, dense, and graph-based baselines (0.501 and 0.460). On DevEval generation, it obtains the highest DIR@1, reaching 52.91\% with DeepSeek-V3.2 and 53.58\% with GPT-5-mini, while maintaining competitive Pass@1 and producing shorter code. Compared with the strongest graph-based baseline, FeatLens reduces graph nodes by 61.0\%, edges by 86.2\%, and total token overhead by 45.9\%, with no LLM tokens used during retrieval.

</details>


### [227] [From Approval to Execution: Reconstruction-Aware Repair Analysis for LLM-Agent Software](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26529)
*Junchi Zhu,Zhenguang Liu,Shaojing Fan,Jianhai Chen,Qinming He*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 论文提出"重建稳定授权"(ReSA)框架,解决LLM智能体审批中检查对象与执行对象版本不一致导致的授权绕过问题,并实现APAS-Finder工具验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM智能体的审批机制以"展示的动作"为保障,但实际执行的对象可能经工作流重载、transcript投影、参数重绑定、持久状态查找等被重建,导致检查与使用之间的对象版本不一致。现有fieldflow与check-coverage分析只能证明字段被检查,无法证明被检查版本真正到达sink、也无法排除其间被替换,故局部完整的修复仍可能遗留授权绕过。

Method: 三项设计:(1)在表示转换、sink依赖、消费版本与授权范围上形式化"重建稳定授权"(ReSA);(2)推导出一组义务(obligations),用于判定候选修复并暴露残余sink后缀;(3)在APAS-Finder中实现分析,并在独立sink oracle观测执行前冻结预测。

Result: 预测与全部28个受控结果一致;四组匹配对在field-flow与check覆盖相同情况下却需相反判定;提供对象流、支配、干扰与同一授权契约时,CodeQL组合可恢复全部8例;两名分析师在4个模型验证判定上全部一致、在20个sink依赖中19个一致;发布消费者上五个修复阻止60/60的越权效果,三个机制对比暴露预测的残余效果。

Conclusion: 修复的充分性取决于实际被消费的"重建后动作",而非仅取决于审批记录或早期被检查的表示。仅验证字段被检查不足以保障授权安全。

Abstract: Approval mechanisms have become a primary safeguard for consequential actions in LLM-agent software. Yet the action shown for approval is often not the object ultimately consumed: workflow reload, transcript projection, argument rebinding, and durable-state lookup may reconstruct it before execution. Existing fieldflow and check-coverage analyses can establish that expected fields were inspected, but not that the inspected object version reaches the sink or that no replacement intervenes between check and use. Consequently, a locally complete repair may still leave a residual authorization bypass after reconstruction. We address this problem through three designs. (1) We formulate reconstruction-stable authorization (ReSA) over representation transitions, sink dependencies, consumed versions, and grant scope. (2) We derive obligations that judge candidate repairs and expose residual sink suffixes. (3) We implement the analysis in APAS-Finder and freeze predictions before an independent sink oracle observes execution. Predictions agree with all 28 controlled outcomes. Four matched pairs require opposite judgments despite identical field-flow and check coverage; a CodeQL composite recovers all eight when supplied object flow, dominance, interference, and the same grant contract. Two analysts agree on all four model-validation dispositions and 19/20 sink dependencies. On released consumers, five repairs prevent 60/60 tested out-of-scope effects, while three mechanism contrasts expose the predicted residual effects. Repair sufficiency therefore depends on the reconstructed action consumed, not merely on an approval record or earlier checked representation.

</details>


### [228] [TraceVIC: Causal Reasoning over Code Evolution for Identifying Vulnerability-Inducing Commits](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26711)
*Fnu Tanish,Samiha Shimmi,Samikshya Chapagain,Hamed Okhravi,Mona Rahimi,Lei Zhang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: TraceVIC：基于时序图的漏洞引入提交（VIC）识别与排序方法，通过建模代码演化来定位根因提交，F2提升显著。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VIC识别依赖git blame与位置启发式（选最早或最近修改），但真正的VIC可能出现在历史任意位置，且漏洞行为可能依赖跨多次修订演化的代码，因此需要推理代码演化而非仅凭位置。

Method: TraceVIC先定位可能的根因行并追踪其跨修订历史，构建图表示以捕捉每个修订内的程序结构及漏洞相关代码的演化；利用时序边保持相邻修订间程序元素的对应关系，对修订历史进行推理，直接依据候选提交对漏洞条件的贡献进行排序。

Result: 消融显示建模完整修订历史将F2从0.637提升至0.814；TraceVIC较SOTA方法F2最高提升28.7%，在四个未见过的C/C++项目中为79个漏洞中的78个识别出有效VIC。

Conclusion: 通过时序图建模代码演化可有效识别和排序VIC，显著优于依赖位置启发式的现有方法。

Abstract: Software vulnerabilities are often discovered long after they are introduced, making it difficult to identify the vulnerability-inducing commit (VIC) responsible for introducing the underlying vulnerable condition. Existing VIC identification techniques largely rely on git blame to trace vulnerable code through revision history and use positional heuristics, such as selecting its earliest or most recent modification. However, the true VIC may occur anywhere within this history, and vulnerable behavior may depend on code that evolves across multiple revisions. We therefore argue that VIC identification requires reasoning about how vulnerability-relevant code evolves, rather than simply where a candidate commit appears in the revision history.
  We present TraceVIC, a temporal graph-based approach for identifying and ranking VICs by reasoning over code evolution. TraceVIC first localizes likely root-cause lines and traces their histories across revisions, constructing graph representations that capture program structure within each revision and the evolution of vulnerability-relevant code across the history. It reasons over the resulting revision history, using temporal edges to preserve correspondences between program elements across consecutive revisions, and directly ranks candidate commits according to their contribution to the vulnerable condition.
  Ablation results show that modeling the full revision history improves F2 from 0.637 to 0.814. TraceVIC improves F2 by up to 28.7% over state-of-the-art methods and identifies a valid VIC for 78 of 79 vulnerabilities across four unseen C/C++ projects.

</details>


### [229] [Metrics Failure in LLM-Based Code Vulnerability Repair: An Empirical Study and a Change-Aware Screen](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26749)
*Om Nepal,Sushant Aryal,Oluseyi Olukola,Nick Rahimi*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 该论文论证编译率（compile rate）不适用于单函数漏洞修复评估，通过5组实验证明其不可靠，并提出change-aware筛选指标diff_F1。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM越来越多用于C/C++安全漏洞的自动修复，而编译率被广泛作为进展的代理指标（生成的补丁是否通过编译）。作者认为该指标在单函数漏洞修复中科学上不可靠，需要更合理的评估方式。

Method: 在Big-Vul的203个漏洞函数上，使用3个开源代码LLM（350M–6.7B参数）和3种提示策略，开展5组受控实验，检验编译率对改进、编译器标准、模型排序、优化目标的响应；并考察whole-function CodeBLEU与提出的diff_F1（只评估编辑区域）。

Result: 编译率：(i)对显著改进代码的干预几乎无反应；(ii)约64%编译失败不归因于模型，且该比例跨模型近乎不变；(iii)同一补丁在单个编译标准flag下变动1.8–2.7倍且无回归；(iv)模型排序与参考相似度指标相反；(v)作为优化目标会奖励非修复（删除/占位式）。whole-function CodeBLEU失效：未修改的漏洞输入得分高于所有模型。diff_F1对no-op给0分，对部分删除式作弊接近0分，但仍奖励真实部分编辑。

Conclusion: 编译率不能作为单函数漏洞修复的进展指标，diff_F1可作为深入执行分析前的廉价筛选工具，但它不是修复质量指标。研究主张采用change-aware、基于执行的评估方法评估LLM漏洞修复。

Abstract: Large language models (LLMs) are increasingly applied to the automated repair of C/C++ security vulnerabilities, and compile rate is a commonly reported proxy for progress: whether the generated patch compiles. We argue that compile rate is a scientifically unreliable metric for single-function vulnerability repair, and we support this with five controlled experiments over 203 vulnerable functions from Big-Vul, three open-source code LLMs (350M to 6.7B parameters), and three prompting strategies. Compile rate (i) barely responds to an intervention that substantially improves the generated code; (ii) is dominated by evaluation-harness and dataset artifacts rather than model quality, with about 64% of compile failures not attributable to the model, a share that is nearly invariant across models; (iii) shifts by 1.8 to 2.7 times on identical patches under a single compiler-standard flag, with zero regressions; (iv) ranks the three models in the opposite order to reference-similarity metrics; and (v) rewards non-repairs when used as an optimization target, since a compiler-feedback loop raises compile rate while similarity to the human fix falls, with manual inspection finding deletion- and placeholder-style non-repairs among the newly compiling outputs. The natural fallback, whole-function CodeBLEU, also fails: an unchanged copy of the vulnerable input outscores every model. We also examine diff_F1, a change-aware screen that scores only the edited region. It gives exactly zero credit to a no-op and near-zero credit to some, though not all, of the deletion-based gaming patches we observed, while still crediting genuine partial edits, so it may serve as a cheap screen before deeper, execution-based analysis. It is not a repair-quality metric, and we report where it falls short. Our findings argue for change-aware, execution-grounded evaluation of LLM-based vulnerability repair.

</details>


<div id='cs.SI'></div>

# cs.SI [[Back]](#toc)

### [230] [User Influence Analysis Based on Blogs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25908)
*Xiang Liu,Yan Jia,Rong Jiang,Yong Quan*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 本文针对社交网络中的信息传播，提出三种基于用户转发微博行为及@符号的用户影响力计算算法，并通过与数据集训练数据对比验证了算法的有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交网络在互联网时代至关重要，用户、政府和企业都依赖其进行信息传播与影响扩散。社交网络可视为以用户为节点、用户关系为链路的信息扩散网络。然而如何准确度量用户影响力是一个关键问题，本文由此出发研究用户影响力计算。

Method: 将社交网络建模为复杂信息扩散网络（用户为节点，用户关系为连接载体）。基于用户在微博中的转发行为以及@符号所体现的关系，提出三种计算用户影响力的算法。

Result: 通过与数据集中训练数据的结果进行对比来评估算法效果，实验表明所提出的算法表现良好。

Conclusion: 基于转发行为和@符号的用户影响力算法能够有效度量用户影响力，在社交网络信息扩散分析中具有实用价值。

Abstract: Rumor and word of mouth spread at the same speed as the highway of information diffusion in the age of the internet. Social networks play quite an important role in the huge internet. Nowadays, social networks have become indispensable in our lives, especially for the government and enterprises. A social network becomes a complex information diffusion network with users working as nodes and the relationships between users working as the vehicle. In this paper, we propose three kinds of algorithms for computing user influence based on the behavior of a user's forwarding microblogs and the symbol of @ in microblogs. We evaluate the effectiveness of the algorithms by comparing the results of our work with the training data in the dataset, and in the end, it proves that our algorithms work well.

</details>


### [231] [Location transparency reduces activity by accounts misrepresenting their location on X](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25933)
*Yuwei Chuai,Thomas Renault,David Rand,Mohsen Mosleh*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: 研究利用X平台2025年11月推出的"About this account"位置披露功能，对8200个声称位于美国的政治活跃账户进行准实验分析，发现位置披露使位置不符账户的发帖活动下降13.1%，主要机制是账户自我约束而非受众行为改变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 冒充本国声音的外国行为者等不真实账户是网络话语争论的核心，但学界对位置声明不准确的账户及其在位置差异被公开后如何行为知之甚少。X平台披露平台推断的运营位置为研究这一问题提供了契机。

Method: 利用X平台2025年11月推出的"About this account"位置披露功能，对8200个声称位于美国的政治活跃账户开展大规模准实验研究，比较披露位置与声称位置匹配和不匹配的账户，覆盖21周内130万条帖子和360万条回复，采用双重差分方法估计因果效应。

Result: 披露前，位置不符账户发布更多误导性、诈骗和加密货币相关内容，但无明显的党派倾向差异。双重差分显示，披露使位置不符账户的发帖量减少13.1%，其中被揭示位于非洲（29.2%）和亚洲（24.4%）的账户降幅最大，带VPN标记、改过用户名或诈骗/加密货币内容较多的账户降幅也较大。回复下降集中在对美国收件人的互动（10.3%），对非美国收件人的回复无显著变化。受众对其帖子的平均参与度无显著变化。

Conclusion: 位置透明度主要通过促使被披露账户自我约束起作用，而非改变受众行为；这些被约束的账户更像是跨境诈骗而非外国政治影响。这表明位置披露是一种限制不真实账户的有效机制，其作用路径在于账户端而非受众端。

Abstract: Concerns about inauthentic accounts, including foreign actors posing as domestic voices, are central to debates about online discourse. Yet, little is known about accounts with inaccurate location claims and how they behave when discrepancies between their claimed and actual locations become publicly visible. In November 2025, X introduced an "About this account" feature that discloses each account's platform-inferred location of operation. We leverage this intervention in a large-scale quasi-experimental study of 8,200 politically engaged accounts claiming a U.S. location, comparing accounts whose disclosed locations matched versus contradicted their claims across 1.3 million posts and 3.6 million replies over 21 weeks. Before disclosure, location-mismatched accounts posted more misleading, scam-related, and cryptocurrency-related content, but showed no distinctive partisan leaning. Difference-in-differences estimates show that disclosure reduced the posting activity of location-mismatched accounts by 13.1% with the largest declines among accounts revealed to be in Africa (29.2%) and Asia (24.4%), and among accounts with VPN flags, username changes, or scam- and crypto-heavy content. Additionally, the decline in their replies was concentrated in interactions with U.S.-based recipients (10.3%), whereas replies to non-U.S.-based recipients showed no statistically significant change. Conversely, there was no significant change in average audience engagement with their posts. Location transparency thus works primarily by inducing restraint among the disclosed accounts rather than by shifting audience behaviour, and the accounts it constrains look at least as much like cross-border fraud as foreign political influence.

</details>


### [232] [Diffusion-Induced Spatial Attention Overlapping Community Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.26737)
*Kosti Koistinen,Vesa Kuikka,Joni Herttuainen,Matthew Hendren,Brian Holt,Kimmo K. Kaski*

Main category: cs.SI

TL;DR: DISCO：结合扩散先验、稀疏多头注意力与伯努利-泊松边重建的深度学习框架，用于重叠社区检测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 重叠社区检测对建模参与多结构/功能群的节点至关重要，但现有GNN依赖局部消息传递，平滑导致社区边界模糊，并限制长程结构依赖的表示。

Method: 融合影响扩散动力学得到的结构先验（识别非直接邻居的候选交互并按结构邻近度偏置注意力）、稀疏多头注意力、非负社区隶属学习；采用伯努利-泊松边重建目标，可从节点属性、结构特征或两者推断重叠社区。

Result: 基准实验中，DISCO在不同输入配置下与主流图卷积和图注意力方法具竞争力；网络安全概念验证中，连续通信网络快照间的社区变化提供可解释异常信号，时间社区相似度识别结构偏差，节点级贡献定位相关设备。

Conclusion: DISCO既提供灵活的重叠社区检测方法，也为动态网络结构变化分析奠定基础。

Abstract: Detection of overlapping communities is essential for modelling networks in which nodes participate simultaneously in multiple structural or functional groups. Existing graph neural network approaches commonly rely on local message passing, which can obscure community boundaries through smoothing and limit the representation of structurally relevant long-range dependencies. We introduce Diffusion-Induced Spatial Attention Community Detection (DISCO), a deep-learning framework that combines a structural prior derived from influence spreading dynamics, sparse multi-head attention, and non-negative community-affiliation learning. The prior identifies candidate interactions beyond immediate graph neighbours and biases attention according to their structural proximity, while a Bernoulli-Poisson edge-reconstruction objective enables overlapping community inference from node attributes and structural profiles, or both. Benchmark experiments show that DISCO performs competitively against established graph convolutional and graph attention approaches across different input configurations. To demonstrate its practical applicability, we present a proof-of-concept cybersecurity use case in which changes between community assignments inferred from consecutive communication-network snapshots provide an interpretable anomaly signal. Temporal community similarity identifies structural deviations, while node-level contributions help locate the devices associated with them. DISCO therefore provides both a flexible method for overlapping community detection and a foundation for analysing structural change in dynamic networks.

</details>


<div id='cs.AI'></div>

# cs.AI [[Back]](#toc)

### [233] [Do Synthetic Personas Predict Real Audience Response? A Sim-to-Real Study Where a No-Persona Baseline Beats Persona-Based Copy Simulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25010)
*Alexandre Cristovão Maiorano*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 利用Upworthy真实A/B测试数据验证：在预测点击率上，无persona的zero-shot基线显著优于十人persona面板，说明合成persona反而降低了预测效度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 营销者日益将LLM作为"synthetic personas"预测受众对文案的反应，但其对真实行为的预测效度及persona机制是否有用尚缺乏实证检验。

Method: 以Upworthy Research Archive（数千个共享真实流量的标题A/B测试及实测点击率）为held-out ground truth，比较基于真实受众人口统计的十人persona面板与无persona的zero-shot基线（直接询问典型读者点击可能性），并检验种子、提示措辞、模型（三代Gemini及OpenAI gpt-4.1）的稳健性。

Result: 真实数据可靠性是首要约束：多数A/B测试无统计显著胜者，仅可靠子集(n=399)可衡量效度。无persona基线排名显著更好(Kendall τ=0.361, top-1 49.2%)，优于persona面板(τ=0.084, top-1 34.6%)，置信区间不重叠；结论在三个Upworthy划分及另一新闻数据集方向上可复现。

Conclusion: 预测聚合参与度时，普通LLM排序器优于persona模拟；合成persona不仅是弱预测器，甚至比不用更差。所有数据可通过公开复现包重新生成。

Abstract: Marketers increasingly use large language models (LLMs) as "synthetic personas" to predict how an audience will react to a piece of copy before it ships, encouraged by evidence that profile-conditioned LLMs mimic human samples. But is that prediction actually valid against real behaviour - and does the persona machinery help? We present a sim-to-real validity study using the Upworthy Research Archive - thousands of headline A/B tests on shared real traffic, with measured click-through - as held-out ground truth. We compare a ten-persona panel, grounded in the real audience's demographics, against a no-persona zero-shot baseline that simply asks the model how likely a typical reader is to click. Two findings stand out. First, ground-truth reliability is the binding constraint: most A/B tests have no statistically distinguishable winner, so validity can only be measured on the reliable subset (n = 399). Second, and counter to the persona-simulation premise, persona conditioning degrades predictive validity: the no-persona baseline ranks variants markedly better (Kendall τ = 0.361, a medium effect; top-1 accuracy 49.2%) than the persona panel (τ = 0.084; top-1 34.6%), with non-overlapping confidence intervals. Asking the model directly taps an accurate population-level prior; forcing it to role-play specific personas injects bias and noise. The result replicates across three independent Upworthy splits, holds in direction on a different-domain news dataset, and is robust to seed, prompt phrasing, and model choice - across three Gemini tiers and a different model family (OpenAI gpt-4.1, significant paired gap). The takeaway: for predicting aggregate engagement, a plain LLM ranker beats persona simulation - synthetic personas are not merely a weak predictor, they are worse than not using them. All numbers regenerate from a public, artifact-first replication package.

</details>


### [234] [Do Existing Preconditioners Improve Biomedical Tabular Foundation Learning? An Empirical Study on TabPFN Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25013)
*M. Sajid,Pinki Khatun,M. Tanveer*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文实证研究了5种基于AdamW的预条件策略在TabPFN v2.5微调中的效果，覆盖59个生物医学数据集，发现原始AdamW综合表现和统计排名最佳。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TabPFN等表格基础模型在结构化生物医学数据分析中展现潜力，但优化与预条件策略对生物医学微调的影响尚未被充分探索。

Method: 对5种基于AdamW的预条件策略进行系统实证比较，在59个生物医学数据集（涵盖阿尔茨海默病、乳腺癌、精神分裂症、SMC、KEEL及UCI基准）上微调TabPFN v2.5，评估预测性能、计算效率与统计显著性。

Result: 原始AdamW在所有策略中综合表现与统计排名一致最优；现有曲率感知预条件器在多样化生物医学场景中无法提供可靠提升。

Conclusion: 通用预条件方法可能难以充分刻画生物医学表格学习的优化特性，需针对医疗导向的表格基础模型开发生物医学感知的预条件器。

Abstract: Tabular foundation models have recently shown strong potential for structured biomedical data analysis. Among them, TabPFN has emerged as an effective approach for low-data tabular classification tasks. However, the impact of optimization and preconditioning strategies on biomedical fine-tuning remains largely unexplored. In this work, we present a comprehensive empirical investigation of five AdamW-based preconditioning strategies for fine-tuning TabPFN v2.5 on 59 biomedical datasets spanning Alzheimer's disease, breast cancer, schizophrenia, significant memory concern (SMC), KEEL biomedical datasets, and UCI biomedical benchmarks. The evaluation considers predictive performance, computational efficiency, and statistical significance analysis. Experimental results demonstrate that the original AdamW optimizer consistently achieves the best overall performance and statistical ranking, while existing curvature-aware preconditioners fail to provide reliable improvements across diverse biomedical learning scenarios. The findings suggest that generic preconditioning approaches may not adequately capture the optimization characteristics of biomedical tabular learning, motivating the development of biomedical-aware preconditioners specifically tailored for healthcare-oriented tabular foundation models.

</details>


### [235] [4DGS-JEPA: Temporally Compositional Joint-Embedding Prediction for Dynamic Gaussian Splatting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25036)
*Yongchao Huang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出4DGS-JEPA，一种面向动态高斯场景的联合嵌入预测架构，实现因果多时域预测，核心原理为时间组合性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有动态高斯泼溅方法主要针对重建、未来状态生成或渲染优化，而非学习可复用的预测动力学。需要一个能学习可复用预测动态的表示方法。

Method: 采用层级表示（场景级、运动组级、高斯级）与以时域为条件的转移算子，支持直接预测与递归展开。核心为时间组合性：不同时序路径到达同一未来端点应产生兼容的预测状态。使用端点与多时域路径监督，配合选择性几何解码器与几何级组合。引入混合对应机制，结合持久规范身份与残差最优传输匹配。

Result: 理论上刻画了零损失路径一致性与有限误差递归累积。三项受控实验证明：时间组合性降低潜在路径依赖同时保持预测精度；几何级组合提升解码运动一致性；混合对应机制在对应模糊时仍保持可靠身份。

Conclusion: 4DGS-JEPA为动态高斯世界提供了预测性、时间组合性的表述框架。

Abstract: Dynamic Gaussian Splatting provides an explicit representation of evolving 3D scenes, but existing approaches are primarily optimized for reconstruction, future-state generation, or rendering rather than for learning reusable predictive dynamics. We propose 4DGS-JEPA, a Gaussian-native joint-embedding predictive architecture for causal multi-horizon prediction over dynamic Gaussian scenes. The model uses a hierarchical scene-, motion-group-, and Gaussian-level representation together with a horizon-conditioned transition operator that supports both direct prediction and recursive rollout. Its central principle is temporal composition: different chronological transition paths reaching the same future endpoint should produce compatible predictive states. Endpoint and multi-horizon path supervision anchor these predictions to future target embeddings, while a selective geometry decoder and geometry-level composition ground the learned dynamics in consistent group motion and Gaussian geometry without requiring complete future appearance reconstruction. We further introduce a hybrid correspondence mechanism that combines persistent canonical identity with residual optimal-transport matching under reordering and topology change. We characterize zero-loss path agreement and finite-error rollout accumulation theoretically. Three controlled experiments provide mechanism-level evidence that temporal composition reduces latent path dependence while retaining predictive accuracy, geometry-level composition improves consistency of decoded motion, and hybrid correspondence preserves reliable identity while remaining robust when correspondence becomes ambiguous. Together, 4DGS-JEPA provides a predictive, temporally compositional formulation of dynamic Gaussian worlds.

</details>


### [236] [An Accurate and Interpretable Hyper Graph Neural Network for GBM Survival Prediction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25088)
*Mushahid Intesum*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出多模态框架，统一sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈层和EST正则，实现GBM生存预测的准确性与可解释性兼得。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有GBM生存预测模型将准确性与可解释性视为竞争目标，高性能模型牺牲透明度，可解释模型降低预测能力；且多依赖单一影像模态，未充分利用多模态MRI与临床元数据的互补信息。

Method: 三组件框架：(1) sheaf超图神经网络，通过有向非对称消息传递捕捉组织块间高阶关系；(2) 概念瓶颈层，将表征压缩为临床概念，实现事前可解释性；(3) 扩展充分性检验(EST)正则项，惩罚不忠实解释。临床与基因组特征经门控融合引入，保留分子标志物的主要预后信号。

Result: 在UPenn-GBM数据集593例患者上5折交叉验证，一致性指数达0.643，在所有对比模型中折间方差最低(std=0.015)。

Conclusion: 首次统一sheaf超图卷积、概念瓶颈监督与EST正则用于脑MRI可解释生存预测，证明准确性与可解释性可兼得。

Abstract: Survival prediction for glioblastoma multiforme (GBM) demands models that are both accurate and interpretable, yet existing approaches treat these objectives as com- peting, where performant models sacrifice transparency, while interpretable models accept degraded predictive power. We argue that this trade-off is not inherent. Graph neural net- works offer a structural foundation for extracting interpretable, explainable representations without compromising discriminative ability. Furthermore, current methods typically rely on a single imaging modality, underutilizing the complementary information available across multi-modal MRI and clinical metadata. We propose a multi-modal framework that inte- grates three components to address both objectives simultaneously: (1) a sheaf hypergraph neural network that captures higher-order relationships among tissue patches through direc- tional, asymmetric message passing; (2) a concept bottleneck layer that compresses learned representations into clinically grounded concepts, enforcing ante-hoc interpretability; and (3) an extension sufficiency test (EST) regularizer that penalizes unfaithful explanations during training, ensuring that model explanations genuinely reflect the internal decision process. Clinical and genomic features are incorporated through gated fusion, preserving the dominant prognostic signal of molecular markers while retaining concept-level traceabil- ity. Evaluated on 593 patients from the UPenn-GBM dataset under 5-fold cross-validation, our framework achieves a concordance index of 0.643 with the lowest fold-level variance among all compared models (std = 0.015). To our knowledge, this is the first work to unify sheaf hypergraph convolution, concept bottleneck supervision, and EST regularization for interpretable survival prediction from brain MRI

</details>


### [237] [Ovis-Embedding: Pushing the Frontiers of Universal Omni-Modal Embeddings](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25165)
*Ovis-Embedding Team*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Ovis-Embedding 是基于共享多模态骨干的全模态嵌入模型家族，统一处理文本、图像、视频和音频，在多个基准上达到 SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有多模态嵌入模型通常为每个模态单独构建编码塔，导致模态割裂（modality fragmentation），难以实现任意模态间的统一检索。

Method: 三大核心设计：(1) 以预训练 Qwen-omni 为骨干，通过低秩初始化的对比训练实现原生全模态初始化；(2) 构建覆盖文本/图像/视频/音频及交错数据的语料，采用同源采样构造任务一致批次以获得有效批内负样本；(3) 用 focal loss 关注难样本，并用相似度蒸馏从专家模型迁移细粒度相似结构，推理时用低秩特征分解实现紧凑灵活维度的嵌入。

Result: 在 MMEB-v3、MMEB-v2、MVEB、MAEB、RTEB 上均取得 SOTA 性能，覆盖文本、图像、视频、音频四种模态。

Conclusion: 统一的全模态训练可克服模态碎片化，推动面向任意模态间检索的通用嵌入模型发展。

Abstract: In this report, we introduce \textbf{Ovis-Embedding}, a state-of-the-art omni-modal embedding family built on native integration of text, image, video, and audio. Instead of assembling separate modality towers, Ovis-Embedding uses a shared multimodal backbone to encode different modalities in a common representation space. Specifically, we make \textbf{three key advances}: (1) \textbf{native omni-modal initialization}: we adopt a pretrained Qwen-omni model as the embedding backbone and adapt it through contrastive training with low-rank initialization; (2) \textbf{data-centric omni-modal training}: we construct a broad, high-quality corpus spanning text, images, video, audio, and interleaved multimodal data. To improve data efficiency, we introduce homogeneous-source sampling to form task-consistent batches with informative in-batch negatives; and (3) \textbf{embedding-specific training and inference optimization}: we use focal loss to emphasize hard examples and similarity-based Embedding Distillation to transfer fine-grained similarity structure from complementary experts. At inference time, low-rank feature decomposition enables compact embeddings with flexible dimensionality and minimal performance loss. Empirical evaluations show that the \textbf{Ovis-Embedding} family achieves state-of-the-art performance on \textbf{MMEB-v3}, \textbf{MMEB-v2}, \textbf{MVEB}, \textbf{MAEB}, and \textbf{RTEB}, demonstrating its effectiveness across text, image, video, and audio modalities. These results highlight the potential of unified omni-modal training to overcome modality fragmentation and advance universal embedding models for any-to-any retrieval.

</details>


### [238] [X-Planner: Event-Structured Task Planning for Embodied Intelligence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25187)
*Howard Lu,Shalfun Li,Porter Pan,Cris,Lumen,Cyril,Eric Hu,Lily Li,Maeve Zhang,Robert Wang,KZ Zheng,Viggo Chen,Tim Ding,Regsis Cheng,YJ Xiao,Kian,Hai Lin,Alan Song,Elise Ma,Gody Li,Victor Yao,Yohann Tang,Ingrid Yu,Jason He,James Wang,Ryan Yu,Ping Yang,Chris Pan,Vincent Chen,Roy Gan,Hao Wang,Qian Wang*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: X-Planner 是一个规划前端，通过层次化标注数据和事件结构化表示改善具身推理的监督与表达，在离线评测与真机执行中均取得优异表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有 VLA 系统把任务规划隐含处理，CoT 规划器依赖粗粒度任务级标注或逐 token 序列化长推理，缺乏对具身推理的有效监督与表示。

Method: 规划数据融合 Ego、UMI 与遥操作，采用层次粒度与源相关标注深度；接管时刻标注和人工设计失败监督在线错误识别。模型侧共享 VLM 主干提供两种事件结构化规划形式：输出可解释事件状态的离散接口，以及通过 Staircase Decoding 在交错 Transformer 深度间传递连续 CoT 状态的隐式接口，并用冻结的隐式到文本重建目标作语义锚。

Result: 离线两步规划评测中，X-Planner 在 BERTScore-F1 与评审 Overall 分数上于四个模型里排名第二；真机实验中分别优于所评估基线。

Conclusion: 结果刻画了规划文本质量与下游执行效果，表明事件结构化规划前端对具身长时程操作有效。

Abstract: Task planning bridges high-level instructions and executable behavior in long-horizon manipulation, yet modern Vision-Language-Action (VLA) systems often leave this intermediate structure implicit. Existing chain-of-thought (CoT) planners also tend to rely on coarse task-level annotations or serialize long reasoning traces token by token. We present X-Planner, a planning front-end that addresses both the supervision and representation of embodied reasoning. Our planning data combine Ego, UMI, and teleoperation under a hierarchy granularity with source-dependent annotation depth. Takeover-time annotations and human-designed failures supervise ongoing error recognition. On the model side, a shared VLM backbone exposes two event-structured plan forms: a discrete interface that emits interpretable event states and a latent interface that relays continuous CoT states across staggered Transformer depths through Staircase Decoding. A frozen latent-to-text reconstruction objective provides a semantic anchor for the latent representation. Offline two-step planning evaluation places X-Planner second among four evaluated models on both BERTScore-F1 and a judge-based Overall score. In real-robot experiments, respectively, outperforming the evaluated baselines. These results characterize planning-text quality and downstream execution.

</details>


### [239] [Lean Pool: An AI-Maintained Archive of Formalized Mathematics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25199)
*Vasily Ilin*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Lean Pool 是一个由 AI 智能体培育、维护和优化的形式化数学库。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 形式化数学库依赖人工构建与维护，成本高、扩展慢；本文探索用 AI 智能体自动生长和优化此类库。

Method: 构建 Lean Pool 仓库，让 AI 智能体负责形式化数学内容的生成、维护与优化。

Result: 摘要未提供具体实验结果或数据。

Conclusion: 提出以 AI 智能体驱动形式化数学库建设的新范式，潜力有待进一步验证。

Abstract: Lean Pool is a repository of formalized mathematics. It is grown, maintained and optimized by AI agents.

</details>


### [240] [The AI Neuroscientist: An Interactive Agentic Interface for Neuroimaging Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25254)
*Aakash Patel,Panos Ketonis,Shreya Saxena,Smita Krishnaswamy,David van Dijk*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出了"AI Neuroscientist"，一个集成大语言模型与神经影像工具集的语言智能体，用于交互式数据分析，并以fNIRS数据进行了演示与基准评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 神经影像数据分析需要专业的编程和统计技能，使得没有计算背景的研究者难以使用，亟需更易访问的分析方式。

Method: 将LLM与神经影像工具集整合，执行质量控制、建模和可视化，允许研究者用自然语言查询数据质量并指定分析参数；使用fNIRS数据演示，并在自建fNIRS基准套件上与带代码沙箱的通用LLM智能体进行对比评估。

Result: 系统提供了一个透明、可交互的替代方案，适用于小规模数据探索；在自建fNIRS基准套件上完成了与通用LLM智能体的对比评估（具体结果摘要未给出）。

Conclusion: 未来将把该架构推广到fMRI等更多模态，并扩展基准套件以覆盖更多fNIRS任务。

Abstract: Analyzing neuroimaging data requires specialized coding and statistical expertise, which limits accessibility for researchers without computational backgrounds. We present the AI Neuroscientist, a language agent for interactive data exploration. The system integrates a large language model (LLM) with a neuroimaging toolset to perform quality control, modeling, and visualization. This allows researchers to query data quality and specify analysis parameters directly in natural language, providing a transparent and interactive alternative to conventional scripted pipelines for small-scale data exploration. We demonstrate these capabilities using functional near-infrared spectroscopy (fNIRS) data, and evaluate the agent on a custom fNIRS benchmarking suite against general-purpose LLM agents with code sandboxes. Future extensions will generalize the architecture to additional modalities, including functional magnetic resonance imaging (fMRI) data, and expand the benchmarking suite to additional fNIRS tasks.

</details>


### [241] [MedGate-Fusion: Integrating First-Encounter Semantic Narratives and Physiological Biomarkers for Prospective Stroke Risk Stratification](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25272)
*Hemn Khdr,Mohammad Noaeen,Karim Keshavjee,Aziz Guergachi,Zahra Shakeri*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出MedGate-Fusion多模态门控架构，融合首诊叙事的Transformer嵌入与10项常规风险标志物，用于初级保健中的前瞻性卒中风险分层，基于CPCSSN电子病历数据构建首诊队列（102,736例）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 初级保健中前瞻性卒中风险分层困难，因为早期风险信号分散在常规生物标志物和非结构化临床叙事中，单一模态难以有效捕捉。

Method: 采用多模态门控架构（MedGate-Fusion），将首诊叙事的Transformer嵌入与10项常规记录的风险标志物整合。数据来自CPCSSN电子病历；从808,921条就诊级观测构建首诊队列，保留102,736例叙事非空且数据足以评估五年卒中结局的独立患者记录。为减少注释中诊断提及导致的显式目标泄漏，在语义编码前对卒中相关术语进行基于词典的脱敏处理。

Result: 构建了含102,736例患者的首诊队列，具备非空临床叙事及可评估五年卒中结局的充分数据（摘要未报告具体性能指标）。

Conclusion: 多模态门控融合方法有潜力利用常规生物标志物与首诊叙事提升初级保健前瞻性卒中风险分层，并通过词典脱敏缓解目标泄漏问题。

Abstract: Prospective stroke risk stratification in primary care is challenging because early risk signals are distributed across routine biomarkers and unstructured clinical narratives. We propose MedGate-Fusion, a multi-modal gated architecture that integrates transformer-based embeddings of first-encounter narratives with ten routinely recorded risk markers. We used electronic medical record data from the Canadian Primary Care Sentinel Surveillance Network (CPCSSN). Starting from 808,921 encounter-level observations, we constructed a first-encounter cohort and retained 102,736 unique patient records with non-empty narratives and sufficient data to evaluate a five-year stroke outcome. To reduce explicit target leakage from diagnostic mentions in notes, we applied dictionary-based redaction of stroke-related terms prior to semantic encoding.

</details>


### [242] [When LLM Agents Fail to Read the Room: ReAdapt for Relational Social Reasoning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25284)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Yunda Liu,Fei Wang,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出ReAdapt框架，在ReAct循环中显式维护结构化社会状态并进行策略化Adapt更新，缓解LLM智能体在关系与内容线索冲突时的表层选择倾向，在社会关系推理基准上显著提升表现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 社交智能体的核心决策（是否回应、该联系谁）本质上依赖人际关系（关系强度、互惠、共同连接）等潜在信息，而非内容显著性。标准LLM智能体循环未显式建模新关系证据如何修正当前社会假设，导致在关系线索与内容线索冲突时倾向于表面明显的选择。

Method: 构建关系推理基准：500个合成社交世界（含好友、关注、互动历史、信息流），生成1000条查询，涵盖反应选择与人脉引荐（寻找最佳桥梁人）两任务；构造中约53%查询的表层明显候选与关系接地的最优解不同，形成"翻转子集"。提出ReAdapt，在ReAct循环中加入结构化社会状态z=(G,B,R,N,D)（目标、信念、关系、规范、披露），每次工具观测后执行类型化Adapt步骤更新状态并输出策略操作（继续、切换、放弃、澄清）再选择下一步动作。

Result: 以Gemini-3-Flash在每任务n=150分层子集上：人脉引荐准确率从37%提升至51%（+14点），反应选择从69%提升至77%（+8点）；Oracle regret分别从0.260降至0.152、从0.095降至0.053。固定模型、工具与环境不变。

Conclusion: 显式的关系状态自适应能帮助LLM智能体将检索到的社会证据转化为修正后的决策，说明关系推理的失败可通过结构化状态建模与策略化更新有效缓解。

Abstract: A social agent's most basic decisions (should I react to this post? who should I reach out to?) are not purely content problems. The right action often hinges on the latent relationship between people -- tie strength, reciprocity, mutual connections -- rather than on which content is most salient. Standard LLM agent loops do not explicitly represent how new relational evidence should revise the agent's current social hypothesis, leaving them prone to surface-obvious choices when relational and content cues diverge. We formalize this failure mode with a relationship-reasoning benchmark: 500 synthetic social worlds with friendships, follows, reaction histories, and feeds, yielding 1,000 queries over two tasks, reaction selection and warm introduction (finding the best bridge to a target person). By construction, the surface-obvious candidate differs from the relationship-grounded oracle in about 53% of queries, forming an overturn subset where the agent must use relational evidence to revise an initially plausible choice. We propose ReAdapt (Relationship-Adaptive Agent with Policy-driven sTate), which augments the ReAct loop with an explicit structured social state z = (G, B, R, N, D) capturing goal, belief, relationship, norm, and disclosure. After each tool observation, ReAdapt runs a typed Adapt step that updates this state and emits a policy operation (continue, switch, abandon, or clarify) before choosing the next action. With Gemini-3-Flash on a stratified subset of n = 150 queries per task, ReAdapt improves warm-introduction accuracy from 37% to 51% (+14 points) and reaction-selection accuracy from 69% to 77% (+8 points). Oracle regret drops from 0.260 to 0.152 and from 0.095 to 0.053, respectively. Holding the model, tools, and environments fixed, these results suggest that explicit relational-state adaptation helps LLM agents turn retrieved social evidence into revised decisions.

</details>


### [243] [Attention as a Routing Graph: Live Circuit Extraction from a Single Forward Pass](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25285)
*Ash Manvi,Samreena Tajreen*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出一种廉价方法：用单次前向传播中的注意力作为路由图，提取指向答案的小规模路径，并验证其因果重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找语言模型中的电路通常需要大量精心设计的干预实验，成本高昂，因此作者希望用更简单的方法近似定位关键路径。

Method: 将注意力视为一次前向传播中的路由图，保留少量指向答案的路由，并通过消融这些边来检验其因果作用；在 induction 和 IOI 任务上，与随机等量边消融对比；在 GPT-2 Small、GPT-2 Medium、Pythia-410M 上每格评估 n=100 个提示，使用配对 gap 检验和 bootstrap 置信区间。

Result: 在已知正确电路的 induction 和 IOI 任务上，消融提取边对模型的损害显著大于消融随机等量边；提取仅需一次前向传播，而逐头 patch sweep 成本约高两个数量级。

Conclusion: 方法不是完整电路图谱，而是一种廉价且能携带真实因果信号的电路草图，在已知任务上有效，同时存在明确失效模式；代码与评估工件已开源。

Abstract: Finding circuits in language models usually means running many careful interventions. We try something simpler: treat attention as a routing map from one forward pass, keep a small set of routes that point toward the answer, and ask whether those routes actually matter.
  They often do. On induction and IOI (tasks where the "right" circuit is already known), ablating our extracted edges hurts the model much more than ablating a random set of the same size. We evaluate n=100 prompts per cell on GPT-2 Small, GPT-2 Medium, and Pythia-410M, with paired gap tests and bootstrap confidence intervals. The extract step costs one forward; a head-by-head patch sweep costs about two orders of magnitude more.
  We are not claiming a complete circuit atlas. We are claiming a cheap sketch that carries real causal signal on known tasks, with clear failure modes when it does not. Code and evaluation artifacts are at https://github.com/Aquinf03/live-circuit-routing.

</details>


### [244] [Learned Enterprise Data Comprehension: Compression and Routing for Data Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25286)
*Ethan Torres,Eric Mills*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"潜在等价学习"框架，将任务相关身份与其数据集相关实现分离，用于企业结构化数据智能体。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 企业结构化数据智能体需在跨schema、关系、策略和业务角色的复杂环境中推理。现有通过markdown记忆/技能文件复用信息的做法掩盖了自然分工：智能体擅长语义推理，学习系统擅长预测和组织重复结构。

Method: 分离持久任务身份与数据集相关实现：支持与反对证据塑造"支持实现的Gaussian原型"以学习身份在特定数据环境中的表达，软隶属度画像保留硬分配丢失的区分；独立的学习查询原型系统表示重复的证据需求，经学习的兼容函数映射到同一持久身份结构，实现身份分解、查询条件化路由。

Result: 在Data Agent Benchmark（54查询、12异构数据集）上，完整实现五次试验达94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1，258/270原始查询成功，对比基准Claude Opus 4.6参考智能体55.51%，提交时在40个榜单条目中排名第一。

Conclusion: 该框架让智能体在已组织的证据状态上运行，而非每次查询重建跨schema结构，显著提升结构化数据任务性能。

Abstract: Structured-data agents in enterprise settings must reason over complex data environments whose relevant evidence is distributed across schemas, relationships, policies, and recurring business roles. Modern agentic systems often address this burden through reusable markdown-style memory or skill files that preserve previously discovered information for later queries, reducing the need to rediscover the same structure repeatedly. This is useful, but it obscures a natural division of labor: agents are well suited to semantic reasoning, while learned systems are well suited to predicting and organizing recurring structure. We introduce latent equivalence learning to bridge this gap. The framework separates persistent task-relevant identities from their dataset-relative realizations. In our realization, supporting and opposing evidence shape support-realized Gaussian prototypes that learn how those identities are expressed in a particular data environment, while soft-membership profiles retain distinctions lost under a hard assignment. A separate learned query-prototype system represents recurring evidential requirements and maps them through a learned compatibility function into the same persistent identity structure. This identity-factorized, query-conditioned routing materializes the relevant dataset-specific evidence for downstream reasoning, allowing the agent to operate over an already organized evidential state rather than reconstructing cross-schema structure at every query. On the Data Agent Benchmark, spanning 54 queries across 12 heterogeneous datasets, our full implementation achieves 94.67% dataset-macro stratified Pass@1 over five complete trials and 258/270 successful raw query attempts, compared with 55.51% for the benchmark's Claude Opus 4.6 reference agent, ranking first among 40 leaderboard entries at submission.

</details>


### [245] [Making Agents More Consistent: Skills Should Form Habits for Repeat Tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25299)
*Travis Weber,Rohit Taneja*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出"技能习惯形成"方法：智能体从自身执行历史中挖掘确定性候选技能，与现有推理路径竞争而非替代，通过四道成本递增的门控筛选，使常见输入走脚本、其余回退到推理。在文本到SQL任务上，习惯化变体在全部456次重复调度中输出一致，且token消耗减少14%–56%，但也揭示了确定性错误的可复现性代价。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 智能体在重复任务上表现不一致（42个任务各跑3次，38%–74%的答案不一致），而买方、审计方和监管方要求一致性。同时智能体极其浪费：95.3%–97.2%的生成内容用于重新推导系统已知的计划。因此需要一种既能保证可审计一致性、又能降低成本的方法。

Method: 技能习惯形成：智能体挖掘自身执行历史得到候选技能（确定性变体），候选与现有路径竞争而非替换；候选声明其覆盖的输入空间区域，常见情况走脚本、其余回退推理。设置四道成本递增的门控，核心门将候选执行轨迹与保留的参考轨迹比对，容差由参考自身运行的波动性决定。此外将路由与参数抽取分离。

Result: 文本到SQL：四个推理臂中三个在42个重复问题中仅11–13次自我复现，第四个26次；而习惯化变体在重复的456次调度中全部复现，且不劣于所替代的每个臂（p<0.0001），token减少14%–56%，在7–53次复用后净收益转正。门控在2.6%的自然改写和26%的边界附近输入上错误放行，13个此类失败中11个在任意阈值下都无法被轨迹一致性门发现。路由与参数抽取分离将端到端准确率从0.888提升到0.952，成本仅为43%。

Conclusion: 习惯形成能同时带来一致性与效率提升，但存在准确率代价：门控会放行本应推迟的工作，且部分失败对轨迹一致性门不可见。确定性错误会精确重复——坏习惯与好习惯同样可靠，这正是换取可审计性的代价。

Abstract: On repeated work, agents are inconsistent. We ran 42 tasks three times each and found that, depending on the model, 38% to 74% returned answers that did not agree. Consistency is what a buyer, an auditor, or a regulator requires, and agents do not have it. They are wasteful too: 95.3% to 97.2% of what an agent generates goes to re-deriving a plan the system already knows.
  We propose skill habit formation. An agent mines its own execution history for candidate skills, deterministic variants that compete against the incumbent rather than replacing it. A candidate declares the region of input space it claims, so the common case runs as a script and the rest falls through to reasoning. Four gates of ascending cost admit candidates; the central one tests a candidate's execution trace against a retained reference, within a tolerance measured from that reference's own run-to-run variability.
  On text-to-SQL, three of four reasoning arms reproduced their own output on 11 to 13 of 42 repeated questions and the fourth on 26 of 42, while a habit-formed variant reproduced on all 456 dispatches we repeated and was non-inferior to every arm it replaced (p<0.0001). It also used 14% to 56% fewer tokens, turning net positive after 7 to 53 reuses.
  We measured what this costs in accuracy. The guard admitted work it should have deferred on 2.6% of natural paraphrases and 26% of inputs near its boundary, and 11 of 13 such failures were invisible to the trace-conformance gate at any threshold. Deterministic errors repeat exactly: a bad habit is as reliable as a good one, and that is the price of the property that makes the system auditable. Separating routing from parameter extraction raised end-to-end accuracy from 0.888 to 0.952 at 43% of the cost.

</details>


### [246] [Potential for Enhanced Learning in Machine Learning Classes by Using Wiki LLM Indexing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25303)
*Brian Wright*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文比较了向量RAG与LLM编译wiki两种知识表示方式。在相同课程语料上，wiki在跨单元概念链接问题上表现更优且更具可溯源性，而向量RAG在单事实问题上表现相当。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型作为课程辅导工具日益普及，但其有效性依赖于与经过审核的教学材料的对接。已有工作发现固定的检索策略并非最优，因此作者想探究是否语料在入库时的结构化方式比查询时的检索量更重要。

Method: 在相同的机器学习课程语料（Foundations of Machine Learning）上，进行受控的正面比较：(A)向量RAG（复现前期最佳配置）；(B)LLM编译wiki（Karpathy框架），在入库时将语料合成为带显式交叉引用和引用的链接概念页面。用59个问题评估，覆盖单事实回忆、跨单元概念链接、综合与解释、以及大纲修订后的时效性，由LLM评审依据人工编写的评分标准打分。

Result: 单事实问题上两种方法相当（9.33 vs 9.96/10）；跨单元链接问题上差异明显：编译wiki保持准确且有根据（9.93分，100%引用来源），而检索得分较低且明显缺乏根据（8.14分，64%）。

Conclusion: wiki的引用可让学生和教师追溯任何论断至其来源讲座，增加了机器学习课程所需的动态检索层。虽然还需进一步测试，但使用AI辅助ML课程学习的教师应考虑基于wiki的结构，因其有潜力支持最佳实践的基础要素。

Abstract: Large language models are increasingly deployed as course-specific tutors, but their usefulness depends on grounding in vetted instructional materials that are often revised mid-semester. Our prior work built a multimodal retrieval-augmented generation (RAG) system over an authentic machine learning course corpus (Foundations of Machine Learning) and found that retrieval improved contextual grounding, but that fixed retrieval strategies were suboptimal. That motivates a different question: whether how a corpus is structured at ingest time matters more than how much is retrieved at query time. We present a controlled head-to-head comparison of two knowledge representations over an identical classroom corpus: (A) vector RAG, replicating the best-performing configuration from our prior study, and (B) an LLM-compiled wiki (Karpathy framework), in which the corpus is synthesized at ingest into linked concept pages with explicit cross-references and citations back to source materials. We evaluate 59 questions spanning single-fact recall, cross-unit concept linking, synthesis and explanation, and currency after a syllabus revision, scored by an LLM judge against a human-authored rubric. Both representations answered single-fact questions about equally well (9.33 vs. 9.96 of 10), but diverged sharply on questions requiring links across course units. The compiled wiki remained accurate and grounded (9.93; 100% grounded in cited sources), while retrieval scored lower and was markedly less grounded (8.14; 64%). The wiki's citations let students and instructors trace any claim back to the lecture that introduced it, adding a layer of dynamic retrieval that machine learning courses require. While further testing is needed, instructors using AI to support learning in ML courses should consider wiki-based structure for its potential to support foundational elements of best practice.

</details>


### [247] [Clarification Is Not Correction: LLMs Fail to Let Go](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25337)
*Jianzhe Lin,Xiaolin Li,Fei Wang,Robert Douglas,Rajeshkumar Golani,Jubin Chheda*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 论文提出"早期后验坍缩"概念：对话失败的根本原因不是记忆问题，而是模型在歧义解决前过早锁定单一解释，使后续澄清沦为"补充上下文"而非"纠正信号"。研究通过受控对话任务验证了这一顺序效应，并呼吁转向"不确定性保留"的状态管理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 对话失败通常被归因于记忆问题（上下文过长、摘要失真、约束遗忘），但作者认为这忽略了更深层的问题：模型并非遗忘，而是过早做出承诺。歧义的早期轮次坍缩为单一隐藏解释后，后续澄清信息被当成额外上下文来"精炼"一个过时状态，而非"推翻"它，因此无法实现有效纠正。

Method: 采用受控对话任务，涵盖写作、规划、编码三类场景，使用Gemini-2.5-Pro和Gemini-2.5-Flash进行数千次试验。通过改变同一信息出现的顺序，对比最终包含相同任务相关信息时的对话结果，检验顺序效应。同时评估标准提示策略与记忆策略（如摘要、思维链）的效果。

Result: 即使最终对话包含等价的任务相关信息，信息顺序不同会导致不同结果（顺序效应）。后续澄清被当作额外上下文而非纠正信号。编码任务尤其脆弱，说明早期假设被嵌入到接口、控制流等结构化产物中。标准提示与记忆策略不可靠：摘要可能加剧歧义坍缩，思维链虽减少推理痕迹中的显式错误承诺，却未提升最终任务成功率。

Conclusion: 若助手无法放弃早期解释，鲁棒性就不能仅依赖事后纠正，而必须防止歧义的早期轮次固化为单一任务状态。研究者应推动交互式LLM研究从"保留更多上下文"转向"保留不确定性"，包括：在歧义存在时保持暂定假设、在高影响歧义持续时先询问再执行、以及在后续证据推翻早期解读时从修正状态重建。

Abstract: Dialogue failures in language models are usually framed as memory failures: context too long, summaries lossy, a constraint forgotten. We argue this misses a deeper problem: in many conversations the model does not forget, it commits too early. An ambiguous early turn collapses into a single hidden interpretation, and later clarification is filtered through that commitment. We call this early posterior collapse: unresolved user intent collapsing into a committed task state before ambiguity is resolved. We study it with controlled dialogue tasks in writing, planning, and coding using Gemini-2.5-Pro and Gemini-2.5-Flash. Across thousands of trials, the same information in different orders yields different outcomes, even when the final dialogue contains equivalent task-relevant information. This order effect suggests later clarification is treated as extra context rather than a corrective signal: it refines a stale task state without invalidating it. Coding tasks are especially vulnerable, suggesting early assumptions get embedded in structured artifacts such as interfaces and control flow. Standard prompting and memory strategies do not reliably help: summaries can collapse ambiguity, and chain-of-thought can reduce explicit wrong commitment in reasoning traces without improving final task success. These findings motivate uncertainty-preserving state management. If assistants cannot let go of early interpretations, robustness cannot rely on post hoc correction alone; it must keep ambiguous early turns from hardening into one task state. Assistants should hold tentative hypotheses while ambiguity remains, ask before executing when high-impact ambiguity persists, and rebuild from a revised state when later evidence invalidates an earlier reading. Rather than one prompting fix, we aim to redirect research for interactive LLMs from retaining more context toward preserving uncertainty.

</details>


### [248] [From Decorative to Load-Bearing: Task Difficulty Shapes the Causal Role of Chain-of-Thought](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25366)
*Renee Jia,Di Mu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 提出"延续式因果测试"来测量CoT对答案的承载程度。发现CoT承载性随任务难度变化：简单任务被绕过，困难任务误差传播严重，且行为模式可读但难控制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: CoT监控的前提是其推理能因果性地约束答案，但"机制忠实性"难以行为层面检验，需要一种区分行为层面承载性的方法。

Method: 延续式因果测试（ablation-patch干预）：扰动某一步推理、截断思维链、强制模型从被破坏的前缀继续。在Gemma-2-9B-IT、Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct、DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B上，于GSM8K、MMLU、BIG-Bench Hard测试；含2x2匹配分析、方差分解（28,584条延续）、评判敏感性四变体分析、双标注者盲审(n=500)、线性探针与激活引导实验。

Result: CoT承载性随模型相对任务难度变化：简单任务模型静默绕过推理，困难任务跟随被破坏步骤并传播误差。误差传播从GSM8K到BBH多步算术上升16倍；方差分解显示98.8%可解释偏差归因于任务难度，仅0.8%归因于扰动类型。推理专用RL抑制误差传播并压缩梯度。误差传播标签对评判提示不变，标注者一致性完美(kappa=1.00)。线性探针可区分静默绕过、自我修正与误差传播，但激活引导最多仅翻转约25%的误差传播案例。

Conclusion: CoT监控存在结构性问题：易读之处信号弱，关键之处误差在监控介入前已传播。行为模式可被读取但无法可靠控制。

Abstract: Chain-of-thought (CoT) monitoring is only meaningful if written reasoning causally constrains the answer. We introduce continuation-based causal testing, an ablation-patch intervention that perturbs one reasoning step, truncates the chain, and forces the model to continue from the corrupted prefix. It measures how load-bearing a CoT is for the final answer, a behavioral notion distinct from mechanistic faithfulness. Across Gemma-2-9B-IT, Llama-3.1-8B-Instruct, and DeepSeek-R1-Distill-Qwen-7B on GSM8K, MMLU, and BIG-Bench Hard, CoT load-bearingness tracks model-relative task difficulty: on easy tasks models silently bypass their own reasoning; on hard tasks they follow corrupted steps and propagate errors. A matched 2x2 analysis shows task difficulty dominates perturbation type: error propagation rises 16x from GSM8K to BBH multistep arithmetic, and a variance partition over 28,584 continuations attributes 98.8% of explained deviance to task difficulty versus 0.8% to perturbation type. Reasoning-specific RL suppresses error propagation and compresses the gradient. A four-variant judge-sensitivity analysis and blind two-annotator study (n=500) show the error-propagation vs. non-propagation label is invariant to judge prompt, with perfect inter-annotator agreement (Cohen's kappa = 1.00). This gradient creates a structural problem for CoT-based oversight and AI safety monitoring: where the trace is easy to read it carries little signal, and where it matters errors propagate before a monitor can intervene. Linear probes on hidden states separate silent bypass, self-correction, and error propagation, but additive activation steering provides limited causal control, flipping only about 25% of error-propagation cases at best. Behavioral mode is readable but not reliably controllable.

</details>


### [249] [Robust Failure, Conservative Repair: Textual Knowledge Distillation from Cross-Model Failures](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25400)
*Andrew Ren,Haokun Liu,Chenhao Tan*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文研究基于失败的文本知识蒸馏，提出RFCR方法，通过跨模型共享失败提取规则、用边界案例锐化应用边界并在无有用规则时弃权，在BIG-Bench Hard上将cheat sheet准确率从68.50%提升至71.25%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基于失败的规则蒸馏存在不稳定性：规则原子可能编码模型特定的推理补丁而混淆其他模型，也可能被误用到无关案例，导致模型因无关信息错误翻转答案；且单模型失败蒸馏在非源模型家族上表现不如基线cheat sheet。

Method: 提出Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR)文本蒸馏流程：从跨模型共享的失败中提取规则，利用边界案例锐化规则的应用边界，并在找不到有用规则时弃权（abstain）。

Result: 在400项BIG-Bench Hard任务集上，RFCR将基线cheat sheet从68.50%提升到71.25%（+2.75个百分点，95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]），且在之前正确的案例上没有性能下降。消融实验和跨模型诊断表明，准确率提升来自新知识注入和严格的规则应用控制两方面。

Conclusion: 跨模型共享失败提取规则并结合严格的应用边界控制，能稳定提升失败驱动知识蒸馏的效果，避免单模型规则带来的负迁移与误用问题。

Abstract: Failure-based textual knowledge distillation aims to discover gaps in a model's knowledge by examining its task errors. The distilled knowledge can be useful for the reasoning of both this model ("source model") and other models. However, this transfer of knowledge may not be stable. We define a rule atom to be a standalone rule injected into a model's textual input at inference time. A rule atom can encode transferable task knowledge or model-specific reasoning patches that can confuse other models. Also, the injected rule atoms can be misapplied to unrelated cases, causing the model to incorrectly flip its answer based on irrelevant information. Building on a pipeline that distills training examples into task-specific cheat sheets that aid model reasoning, we examine when failure-derived rules can improve these cheat sheets. Our early experiment shows rule distillation from a single model's failures underperforms the baseline cheat sheet on non-source model families. This motivates Robust Failure, Conservative Repair (RFCR), a textual distillation procedure that derives rules from failures shared across models, sharpens their application boundaries using boundary cases, and abstains when no useful rule is found. On a 400-item BIG-Bench Hard task set, RFCR improves the baseline cheat sheets from 68.50% to 71.25% (+2.75 pp; 95% CI [+1.25,+4.50]) without performance degradation on previously correct cases. Ablations and cross-model diagnostics support that accuracy gains come from both new knowledge injection and strict rule-application control.

</details>


### [250] [Efficient Iterative Retrieval with Heterogeneous Batching](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25405)
*Dohyun Park,Hubertus Franke,Daniel G. Waddington,Swaminathan Sundararaman,Yongjoo Park*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: Orthrus是一个统一推理循环的异构批处理服务系统，解决嵌入与生成模型单独执行导致的低吞吐和GPU利用率低问题，在4×A100上实现1.28–4.52倍吞吐提升和最高55.8%的p99延迟降低。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代信息检索需要同时使用嵌入模型和生成模型处理复杂查询，但现有服务系统将两者隔离执行，导致吞吐低、GPU利用率差；粗粒度分区无法适应动态负载并产生计算"气泡"。

Method: Orthrus在统一推理循环中执行异构批处理。针对嵌入与生成计算模式冲突的挑战，采用分块嵌入（chunked embedding）配合增量池化（incremental pooling），并以负载感知的方式调整批组合。

Result: 在4块A100 GPU上，相比基线部署，Orthrus在受控负载上吞吐提升1.28–4.52倍，在迭代式RAG基准上端到端p99延迟最多降低55.8%。代码已开源。

Conclusion: 通过统一推理循环的异构批处理，Orthrus有效提升了嵌入与生成混合负载的服务吞吐和GPU利用率，并显著降低延迟。

Abstract: Modern information retrieval increasingly employs both embedding and generative models to handle complex queries. However, current serving systems suffer from low throughput and poor GPU utilization because they execute these models in isolation. Coarse-grained partitioning, such as dedicating GPUs to specific tasks, fails to adapt to dynamic workloads and creates computational "bubbles". To address these, we present Orthrus, a serving system that performs heterogeneous batching within a unified inference loop. The primary challenge lies in unifying embedding and generation workloads with conflicting computational patterns while optimizing batch composition for high performance. Orthrus addresses these challenges through chunked embedding with incremental pooling and by adjusting batch composition in a workload-aware manner. Evaluation on four A100 GPUs shows that, relative to baseline deployments, Orthrus achieves 1.28$\times$--4.52$\times$ higher throughput on controlled workloads and up to 55.8% lower end-to-end p99 latency on an iterative-RAG benchmark. We release our code at https://github.com/illinoisdata/Orthrus .

</details>


### [251] [From Offline Proxies to Online Decisions: A Layered Engagement Evaluation Framework for Conversational AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.25408)
*Xuanyi Li,Vaskar Nath,Hossein Amirkhani,Jay Li,Alex Deng*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 该论文提出一种可复用的离线代理构建与诊断方法，用于在稀缺的在线A/B实验前筛选用对话AI候选方案，并通过冻结映射后的实验验证其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在线A/B实验是用户参与度决策标准，但流量和读出时间限制了可测试的对话AI改动数量，因此需要无需实验组用户暴露的离线信号来提前筛选候选方案。

Method: 提出可复用构建与诊断清单，将离线代理视为三层对齐链条：行为标签到产品结果、学习分类器到候选助手行为、聚合离线信号到实验效应。配套评估协议通过区间感知决策一致性（比较离线与在线置信区间）和实验内排序审计整体复合体。实例包括固定评估套件、预测会话/提示级参与度的分类器，以及将样本级分数差映射到在线模型级参与度增量的校准层。

Result: 在已部署多轮助手的27个实验中报告489对离线-在线对照。主要测试使用映射冻结后8个实验的113对对照：该复合体达到81.1% F1，而其所基于的原始分类器分数为34.3%；复合体没有错误方向判断，而原始分数有31次。所有离线预测均在实验运行前计算以防过拟合。

Conclusion: 证据支持在稀缺实验流量分配前使用该复合体优先排序候选方案，在部署实验中用于选择训练检查点和调优系统提示。

Abstract: Online A/B experiments are the decision standard for user engagement, but traffic and readout time limit how many conversational-AI changes can be tested. We ask whether an offline signal designed to be computable without treatment-arm user exposure agrees with the outcomes of those experiments. We contribute a reusable construction and diagnosis checklist that treats an offline proxy as a chain of three alignments: behavioral label to product outcome, learned classifier to candidate-assistant behavior, and aggregated offline signal to experiment effect. A companion evaluation protocol audits the whole composite by interval-aware decision agreement, which compares offline and online confidence intervals instead of point estimates, and by within-experiment ranking. The instantiation we evaluate comprises a fixed evaluation suite on which candidate behavior is scored, an engagement classifier trained to predict session/prompt level engagements, and a calibration layer mapping sample-level score differences to online model-level engagement deltas. We then report the audit: 489 paired offline-online contrasts (one candidate arm against its control) from 27 experiments on a deployed multi-turn assistant, spanning model checkpoints to system-prompt tuning. Our primary test uses the 113 contrasts from eight experiments that ran after the map was frozen: on these the composite reaches 81.1% F1, against 34.3% for the raw classifier score it is built on, and makes no wrong-direction calls where that raw score makes 31. Every offline prediction was computed before its experiment ran to prevent overfitting. The evidence supports using the composite to prioritize candidates before scarce experiment traffic is allocated---in our deployment of the experiment, selecting among training checkpoints and tuning system prompts.

</details>
